# Plan: Add Factory AI Droid Integration Tests

## Context

The integration test folder (`cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/`) has comprehensive tests for Claude Code and Gemini CLI agents — detection, hook installation, session operations, strategy composition, and `enable` command smoke tests. Factory AI Droid has **zero** integration tests despite implementing `HookSupport`, `HookHandler`, `TranscriptAnalyzer`, and `SubagentAwareExtractor`. This is a coverage gap for the real CLI binary execution path.

## Files to Modify/Create

| File | Action | Purpose |
|------|--------|---------|
| `integration_test/hooks.go` | Modify | Add `FactoryDroidHookRunner`, `FactoryDroidSession`, `CreateDroidTranscript` |
| `integration_test/agent_test.go` | Modify | Add detection, hook install, helper, and session stub tests |
| `integration_test/setup_factoryai_hooks_test.go` | Create | Smoke tests for `entire enable --agent factoryai-droid` |
| `integration_test/agent_strategy_test.go` | Modify | Add agent+strategy composition tests |

## Step 1: Add Hook Runner to `hooks.go`

Add `FactoryDroidHookRunner` mirroring `GeminiHookRunner` (line 501).

**Key details:**
- Routes to `exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "factoryai-droid", hookName)`
- No project dir env var needed (unlike Claude/Gemini, `GetSessionDir` is a stub)
- Just passes `os.Environ()` and `cmd.Dir = r.RepoDir`

**Simulation methods:**
- `SimulateUserPromptSubmit(sessionID)` — input: `{session_id, transcript_path, prompt}`
- `SimulateStop(sessionID, transcriptPath)` — input: `{session_id, transcript_path}`
- `SimulateSessionStart(sessionID)` — input: `{session_id, transcript_path}`
- `SimulateSessionEnd(sessionID, transcriptPath)` — input: `{session_id, transcript_path}`
- `SimulatePreTask(sessionID, transcriptPath, toolUseID)` — input with `tool_input`
- `SimulatePostTask(input PostTaskInput)` — input with `tool_response.agentId`
- `SimulateUserPromptSubmitWithOutput`, `SimulateSessionStartWithOutput` — return `HookOutput`

**`FactoryDroidSession` struct + `NewFactoryDroidSession()` on TestEnv:**
- Session ID format: `"droid-session-N"`
- Transcript path: `.entire/tmp/<id>.jsonl`
- `CreateDroidTranscript(prompt, changes)` — must generate **Droid envelope JSONL**, not Claude format

**Droid transcript format** (critical — `ParseDroidTranscript` requires `type:"message"` outer wrapper):
```jsonl
{"type":"message","id":"m1","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Fix the bug"}]}}
{"type":"message","id":"m2","message":{"role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I'll fix it"},{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_1","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"main.go","content":"..."}}]}}
{"type":"message","id":"m3","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"toolu_1","content":"Success"}]}}
```

**Convenience methods on TestEnv:**
- `SimulateFactoryDroid{UserPromptSubmit,Stop,SessionStart,SessionEnd,PreTask,PostTask}`
- `SimulateFactoryDroid{UserPromptSubmit,SessionStart}WithOutput`

## Step 2: Add Tests to `agent_test.go`

Add 4 test functions at the end. Import `factoryaidroid` package.

### `TestFactoryAIDroidAgentDetection` (not parallel — `os.Chdir`)
- "agent is registered" — `agent.List()` contains `"factoryai-droid"` (parallel)
- "detects presence when .factory exists" — create `.factory` dir, `os.Chdir`, `DetectPresence()` → true

### `TestFactoryAIDroidHookInstallation` (not parallel — `os.Chdir`)
- "installs all required hooks" — `InstallHooks(false, false)` returns 7; `AreHooksInstalled()` true; settings.json contains SessionStart, SessionEnd, Stop, UserPromptSubmit, PreToolUse, PostToolUse, PreCompact; permissions.deny has `Read(./.entire/metadata/**)`
- "idempotent - second install returns 0"
- "localDev mode uses go run" — settings.json contains `"go run"` and `"${FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR}"`
- "production mode uses entire binary" — settings.json contains `"entire hooks factoryai-droid"`
- "force flag reinstalls hooks" — force=true returns 7 after initial install

### `TestFactoryAIDroidHelperMethods` (parallel)
- "FormatResumeCommand returns droid --session-id" — `FormatResumeCommand("abc123")` == `"droid --session-id abc123"`
- "GetHookConfigPath returns .factory/settings.json"

### `TestFactoryAIDroidSessionStubs` (parallel)
- "ReadSession returns not-implemented error"
- "WriteSession returns not-implemented error"
- "GetSessionDir returns not-implemented error"

## Step 4: Add Strategy Tests to `agent_strategy_test.go`

### `TestFactoryAIDroidAgentStrategyComposition`
- Iterate `AllStrategies()` manually (can't use `RunForAllStrategies` — it defaults agent to claude-code)
- For each strategy: `NewTestEnv` → `InitRepo` → `InitEntireWithAgent(strat, "factoryai-droid")` → initial commit → feature branch

- Simulate `UserPromptSubmit` → `Stop`
- Verify `GetRewindPoints()` has >= 1 point

    27→// NewHookRunner creates a new hook runner for the given repo directory.

    29→	Helper()
    30→	Fatalf(format string, args ...interface{})
    31→	Logf(format string, args ...interface{})
    32→}) *HookRunner {
    33→	return &HookRunner{
    34→		RepoDir:          repoDir,
    35→		ClaudeProjectDir: claudeProjectDir,

    40→// HookResponse represents the JSON response from Claude Code hooks.

    44→}
    45→
    46→// SimulateUserPromptSubmit simulates the UserPromptSubmit hook.
    47→// This captures pre-prompt state (untracked files).
    48→func (r *HookRunner) SimulateUserPromptSubmit(sessionID string) error {
    49→	r.T.Helper()
    50→
    51→	input := map[string]string{
    52→		"session_id":      sessionID,
    53→		"transcript_path": "", // Not used for user-prompt-submit
    54→	}
    55→
    56→	return r.runHookWithInput("user-prompt-submit", input)
    57→}
    58→
    59→// SimulateUserPromptSubmitWithTranscriptPath simulates the UserPromptSubmit hook
    60→// with an explicit transcript path. This is needed for mid-session commit detection
    61→// which reads the live transcript to detect ongoing sessions.
    62→func (r *HookRunner) SimulateUserPromptSubmitWithTranscriptPath(sessionID, transcriptPath string) error {
    63→	r.T.Helper()
    64→
    65→	input := map[string]string{
    66→		"session_id":      sessionID,
    67→		"transcript_path": transcriptPath,
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	return r.runHookWithInput("user-prompt-submit", input)
    71→}
    72→
    73→// SimulateUserPromptSubmitWithResponse simulates the UserPromptSubmit hook
    74→// and returns the parsed hook response (for testing blocking behavior).
    75→func (r *HookRunner) SimulateUserPromptSubmitWithResponse(sessionID string) (*HookResponse, error) {
    76→	r.T.Helper()
    77→
    78→	input := map[string]string{
    79→		"session_id":      sessionID,
    80→		"transcript_path": "", // Not used for user-prompt-submit
    81→	}
    82→
    83→	inputJSON, err := json.Marshal(input)
    84→	if err != nil {
    85→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hook input: %w", err)
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	output := r.runHookWithOutput("user-prompt-submit", inputJSON)
    89→
    90→	// If hook failed with an error, return the error
    91→	if output.Err != nil {
    92→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("hook failed: %w\nStderr: %s\nStdout: %s",
    93→			output.Err, output.Stderr, output.Stdout)
    94→	}
    95→
    96→	// Parse JSON response from stdout
    97→	var resp HookResponse
    98→	if len(output.Stdout) > 0 {
    99→		if err := json.Unmarshal(output.Stdout, &resp); err != nil {
   100→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook response: %w\nStdout: %s",
   101→				err, output.Stdout)
   102→		}
   103→	}
   104→
   105→	return &resp, nil
   106→}

   195→func (r *HookRunner) runHookWithInput(flag string, input interface{}) error {
   196→	r.T.Helper()
   197→
   198→	inputJSON, err := json.Marshal(input)
   199→	if err != nil {
   200→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hook input: %w", err)
   201→	}
   202→
   203→	return r.runHookInRepoDir(flag, inputJSON)
   204→}
   205→
   206→func (r *HookRunner) runHookInRepoDir(hookName string, inputJSON []byte) error {
   207→	// Run using the shared test binary
   208→	// Command structure: entire hooks claude-code <hook-name>
   209→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "claude-code", hookName)
   210→	cmd.Dir = r.RepoDir
   211→	cmd.Stdin = bytes.NewReader(inputJSON)
   212→	cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   213→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+r.ClaudeProjectDir,
   214→	)
   215→
   216→	output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
   217→	if err != nil {
   218→		return fmt.Errorf("hook %s failed: %w\nInput: %s\nOutput: %s",
   219→			hookName, err, inputJSON, output)
   220→	}
   221→
   222→	r.T.Logf("Hook %s output: %s", hookName, output)
   223→	return nil
   224→}

   226→// Session represents a simulated Claude Code session.
   227→type Session struct {
   228→	ID                string // Raw model session ID (e.g., "test-session-1")
   229→	TranscriptPath    string
   230→	TranscriptBuilder *TranscriptBuilder
   231→	env               *TestEnv
   232→}
   233→
   234→// FileChange represents a file modification in a session.
   235→type FileChange struct {
   236→	Path    string
   237→	Content string
   238→}
   239→
   240→// NewSession creates a new simulated session.
   241→func (env *TestEnv) NewSession() *Session {
   242→	env.T.Helper()
   243→
   244→	env.SessionCounter++
   245→	sessionID := fmt.Sprintf("test-session-%d", env.SessionCounter)
   246→
   247→	transcriptPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".entire", "tmp", sessionID+".jsonl")
   248→
   249→	return &Session{
   250→		ID:                sessionID,
   251→		TranscriptPath:    transcriptPath,
   252→		TranscriptBuilder: NewTranscriptBuilder(),
   253→		env:               env,
   254→	}
   255→}
   256→
   257→// CreateTranscript builds and writes a transcript for the session.
   258→func (s *Session) CreateTranscript(prompt string, changes []FileChange) string {
   259→	s.TranscriptBuilder.AddUserMessage(prompt)
   260→	s.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("I'll help you with that.")
   261→
   262→	for _, change := range changes {
   263→		toolID := s.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolUse("mcp__acp__Write", change.Path, change.Content)
   264→		s.TranscriptBuilder.AddToolResult(toolID)
   265→	}
   266→
   267→	s.TranscriptBuilder.AddAssistantMessage("Done!")
   268→
   269→	if err := s.TranscriptBuilder.WriteToFile(s.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
   270→		s.env.T.Fatalf("failed to write transcript: %v", err)
   271→	}
   272→
   273→	return s.TranscriptPath
   274→}

   366→// ClearSessionState removes the session state file for the given session ID.
   367→// This simulates what happens when a user commits their changes (session is "completed").
   368→// Used in tests to allow sequential sessions to run without triggering concurrent session warnings.
   369→func (env *TestEnv) ClearSessionState(sessionID string) error {
   370→	env.T.Helper()
   371→
   372→	// Session state is stored in .git/entire-sessions/<session-id>.json
   373→	stateFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "entire-sessions", sessionID+".json")
   374→
   375→	if err := os.Remove(stateFile); err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {
   376→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to clear session state: %w", err)
   377→	}
   378→	return nil
   379→}
   380→
   381→// HookOutput contains the result of running a hook.
   382→type HookOutput struct {
   383→	Stdout []byte
   384→	Stderr []byte
   385→	Err    error
   386→}
   387→
   388→// runHookWithOutput runs a hook and returns both stdout and stderr separately.
   389→func (r *HookRunner) runHookWithOutput(hookName string, inputJSON []byte) HookOutput {
   390→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "claude-code", hookName)
   391→	cmd.Dir = r.RepoDir
   392→	cmd.Stdin = bytes.NewReader(inputJSON)
   393→	cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   394→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+r.ClaudeProjectDir,
   395→	)
   396→
   397→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   398→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   399→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   400→
   401→	err := cmd.Run()
   402→	return HookOutput{
   403→		Stdout: stdout.Bytes(),
   404→		Stderr: stderr.Bytes(),
   405→		Err:    err,
   406→	}
   407→}

   457→// GetSessionState reads and returns the session state for the given session ID.
   458→func (env *TestEnv) GetSessionState(sessionID string) (*strategy.SessionState, error) {
   459→	env.T.Helper()
   460→
   461→	stateFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "entire-sessions", sessionID+".json")
   462→
   463→	data, err := os.ReadFile(stateFile)
   464→	if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   465→		return nil, nil
   466→	}
   467→	if err != nil {
   468→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session state: %w", err)
   469→	}
   470→
   471→	var state strategy.SessionState
   472→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
   473→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse session state: %w", err)
   474→	}
   475→	return &state, nil

   478→// WriteSessionState writes the session state for the given session ID.

   486→	}
   487→

   489→

   491→	if err != nil {

   493→	}

   495→	if err := os.WriteFile(stateFile, data, 0o644); err != nil {
   496→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write session state: %w", err)

   498→	return nil

   501→// GeminiHookRunner executes Gemini CLI hooks in the test environment.
   502→type GeminiHookRunner struct {
   503→	RepoDir          string
   504→	GeminiProjectDir string
   505→	T                interface {
   506→		Helper()
   507→		Fatalf(format string, args ...interface{})
   508→		Logf(format string, args ...interface{})

   510→}
   511→
   512→// NewGeminiHookRunner creates a new Gemini hook runner for the given repo directory.
   513→func NewGeminiHookRunner(repoDir, geminiProjectDir string, t interface {
   514→	Helper()
   515→	Fatalf(format string, args ...interface{})
   516→	Logf(format string, args ...interface{})
   517→}) *GeminiHookRunner {
   518→	return &GeminiHookRunner{
   519→		RepoDir:          repoDir,
   520→		GeminiProjectDir: geminiProjectDir,
   521→		T:                t,
   522→	}
   523→}
   524→
   525→// runGeminiHookWithInput runs a Gemini hook with the given input.
   526→func (r *GeminiHookRunner) runGeminiHookWithInput(hookName string, input interface{}) error {
   527→	r.T.Helper()
   528→
   529→	inputJSON, err := json.Marshal(input)
   530→	if err != nil {
   531→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hook input: %w", err)
   532→	}
   533→
   534→	return r.runGeminiHookInRepoDir(hookName, inputJSON)
   535→}
   536→
   537→func (r *GeminiHookRunner) runGeminiHookInRepoDir(hookName string, inputJSON []byte) error {
   538→	// Run using the shared test binary
   539→	// Command structure: entire hooks gemini <hook-name>
   540→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "gemini", hookName)
   541→	cmd.Dir = r.RepoDir
   542→	cmd.Stdin = bytes.NewReader(inputJSON)
   543→	cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   544→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+r.GeminiProjectDir,
   545→	)
   546→
   547→	output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
   548→	if err != nil {
   549→		return fmt.Errorf("hook %s failed: %w\nInput: %s\nOutput: %s",
   550→			hookName, err, inputJSON, output)
   551→	}
   552→
   553→	r.T.Logf("Gemini hook %s output: %s", hookName, output)
   554→	return nil
   555→}
   556→
   557→// runGeminiHookWithOutput runs a Gemini hook and returns both stdout and stderr separately.
   558→func (r *GeminiHookRunner) runGeminiHookWithOutput(hookName string, inputJSON []byte) HookOutput {
   559→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "gemini", hookName)
   560→	cmd.Dir = r.RepoDir
   561→	cmd.Stdin = bytes.NewReader(inputJSON)
   562→	cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   563→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+r.GeminiProjectDir,
   564→	)
   565→
   566→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   567→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   568→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   569→
   570→	err := cmd.Run()
   571→	return HookOutput{
   572→		Stdout: stdout.Bytes(),
   573→		Stderr: stderr.Bytes(),
   574→		Err:    err,
   575→	}
   576→}

   649→// GeminiSession represents a simulated Gemini CLI session.
   650→type GeminiSession struct {
   651→	ID             string // Raw model session ID (e.g., "gemini-session-1")
   652→	TranscriptPath string
   653→	env            *TestEnv
   654→}
   655→
   656→// NewGeminiSession creates a new simulated Gemini session.
   657→func (env *TestEnv) NewGeminiSession() *GeminiSession {
   658→	env.T.Helper()
   659→
   660→	env.SessionCounter++
   661→	sessionID := fmt.Sprintf("gemini-session-%d", env.SessionCounter)
   662→	transcriptPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".entire", "tmp", sessionID+".json")
   663→
   664→	return &GeminiSession{
   665→		ID:             sessionID,
   666→		TranscriptPath: transcriptPath,
   667→		env:            env,
   668→	}
   669→}
   670→
   671→// CreateGeminiTranscript creates a Gemini JSON transcript file for the session.
   672→func (s *GeminiSession) CreateGeminiTranscript(prompt string, changes []FileChange) string {
   673→	// Build Gemini-format transcript (JSON, not JSONL)
   674→	messages := []map[string]interface{}{
   675→		{
   676→			"type":    "user",
   677→			"content": prompt,
   678→		},
   679→		{
   680→			"type":    "assistant",
   681→			"content": "I'll help you with that.",
   682→		},
   683→	}
   684→
   685→	for _, change := range changes {
   686→		messages = append(messages, map[string]interface{}{
   687→			"type": "tool_use",
   688→			"name": "write_file",
   689→			"input": map[string]string{
   690→				"path":    change.Path,
   691→				"content": change.Content,
   692→			},
   693→		})
   694→		messages = append(messages, map[string]interface{}{
   695→			"type":   "tool_result",
   696→			"output": "File written successfully",
   697→		})
   698→	}
   699→
   700→	messages = append(messages, map[string]interface{}{
   701→		"type":    "assistant",
   702→		"content": "Done!",
   703→	})
   704→
   705→	transcript := map[string]interface{}{
   706→		"sessionId": s.ID,
   707→		"messages":  messages,
   708→	}
   709→
   710→	// Ensure directory exists
   711→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(s.TranscriptPath), 0o755); err != nil {
   712→		s.env.T.Fatalf("failed to create transcript dir: %v", err)
   713→	}
   714→
   715→	// Write transcript
   716→	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(transcript, "", "  ")
   717→	if err != nil {
   718→		s.env.T.Fatalf("failed to marshal transcript: %v", err)
   719→	}
   720→	if err := os.WriteFile(s.TranscriptPath, data, 0o644); err != nil {
   721→		s.env.T.Fatalf("failed to write transcript: %v", err)
   722→	}
   723→
   724→	return s.TranscriptPath
   725→}

    17→// TestAgentDetection verifies agent detection and default behavior.
    18→// Not parallel - contains subtests that use os.Chdir which is process-global.
    19→func TestAgentDetection(t *testing.T) {
    20→
    21→	t.Run("defaults to claude-code when nothing configured", func(t *testing.T) {

    29→			t.Fatalf("Get(default) error = %v", err)
    30→		}
    31→		if ag.Name() != "claude-code" {
    32→			t.Errorf("default agent = %q, want %q", ag.Name(), "claude-code")
    33→		}
    34→	})

    42→		claudeDir := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude")

    46→		settingsPath := filepath.Join(claudeDir, claudecode.ClaudeSettingsFileName)

    89→// TestAgentHookInstallation verifies hook installation via agent interface.
    90→// Note: These tests cannot run in parallel because they use os.Chdir which affects the entire process.
    91→func TestAgentHookInstallation(t *testing.T) {

   110→

   114→		}
   115→
   116→		count, err := hookAgent.InstallHooks(false, false)
   117→		if err != nil {
   118→			t.Fatalf("InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
   119→		}
   120→
   121→		// Should install 7 hooks: SessionStart, SessionEnd, Stop, UserPromptSubmit, PreToolUse[Task], PostToolUse[Task], PostToolUse[TodoWrite]
   122→		if count != 7 {

   124→		}
   125→
   126→		// Verify hooks are installed

   131→		// Verify settings.json was created

   134→			t.Error("settings.json was not created")

   136→
   137→		// Verify permissions.deny contains metadata deny rule

   142→		content := string(data)

   206→			t.Error("localDev hooks should use 'go run', but settings.json doesn't contain it")
   207→		}
   208→	})
   209→}

   211→// TestAgentSessionOperations verifies ReadSession/WriteSession via agent interface.
   212→func TestAgentSessionOperations(t *testing.T) {
   213→	t.Parallel()
   214→
   215→	t.Run("ReadSession parses transcript and computes ModifiedFiles", func(t *testing.T) {
   216→		t.Parallel()
   217→		env := NewTestEnv(t)
   218→		env.InitRepo()
   219→
   220→		// Create a transcript file
   221→		transcriptPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, "test-transcript.jsonl")
   222→		transcriptContent := `{"type":"user","uuid":"u1","message":{"content":"Fix the bug"}}
   223→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a1","message":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"I'll fix it"},{"type":"tool_use","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"main.go"}}]}}
   224→{"type":"user","uuid":"u2","message":{"content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"a1"}]}}
   225→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a2","message":{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","name":"Edit","input":{"file_path":"util.go"}}]}}
   226→`
   227→		if err := os.WriteFile(transcriptPath, []byte(transcriptContent), 0o644); err != nil {
   228→			t.Fatalf("failed to write transcript: %v", err)
   229→		}
   230→
   231→		ag, _ := agent.Get("claude-code")
   232→		session, err := ag.ReadSession(&agent.HookInput{
   233→			SessionID:  "test-session",
   234→			SessionRef: transcriptPath,
   235→		})
   236→		if err != nil {
   237→			t.Fatalf("ReadSession() error = %v", err)
   238→		}
   239→
   240→		// Verify session metadata
   241→		if session.SessionID != "test-session" {
   242→			t.Errorf("SessionID = %q, want %q", session.SessionID, "test-session")
   243→		}
   244→		if session.AgentName != "claude-code" {
   245→			t.Errorf("AgentName = %q, want %q", session.AgentName, "claude-code")
   246→		}
   247→
   248→		// Verify NativeData is populated
   249→		if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   250→			t.Error("NativeData is empty, want transcript content")
   251→		}
   252→
   253→		// Verify ModifiedFiles computed
   254→		if len(session.ModifiedFiles) != 2 {
   255→			t.Errorf("ModifiedFiles = %v, want 2 files (main.go, util.go)", session.ModifiedFiles)
   256→		}
   257→	})
   258→
   259→	t.Run("WriteSession writes NativeData to file", func(t *testing.T) {
   260→		t.Parallel()
   261→		env := NewTestEnv(t)
   262→		env.InitRepo()
   263→
   264→		ag, _ := agent.Get("claude-code")
   265→
   266→		// First read a session
   267→		srcPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, "src.jsonl")
   268→		srcContent := `{"type":"user","uuid":"u1","message":{"content":"hello"}}
   269→`
   270→		if err := os.WriteFile(srcPath, []byte(srcContent), 0o644); err != nil {
   271→			t.Fatalf("failed to write source: %v", err)
   272→		}
   273→
   274→		session, _ := ag.ReadSession(&agent.HookInput{
   275→			SessionID:  "test",
   276→			SessionRef: srcPath,
   277→		})
   278→
   279→		// Write to a new location
   280→		dstPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, "dst.jsonl")
   281→		session.SessionRef = dstPath
   282→
   283→		if err := ag.WriteSession(session); err != nil {
   284→			t.Fatalf("WriteSession() error = %v", err)
   285→		}
   286→
   287→		// Verify file was written
   288→		data, err := os.ReadFile(dstPath)
   289→		if err != nil {
   290→			t.Fatalf("failed to read destination: %v", err)
   291→		}
   292→		if string(data) != srcContent {
   293→			t.Errorf("written content = %q, want %q", string(data), srcContent)
   294→		}
   295→	})
   296→
   297→	t.Run("WriteSession rejects wrong agent", func(t *testing.T) {
   298→		t.Parallel()
   299→
   300→		ag, _ := agent.Get("claude-code")
   301→
   302→		session := &agent.AgentSession{
   303→			SessionID:  "test",
   304→			AgentName:  "other-agent", // Wrong agent
   305→			SessionRef: "/tmp/test.jsonl",
   306→			NativeData: []byte("data"),
   307→		}
   308→
   309→		err := ag.WriteSession(session)
   310→		if err == nil {
   311→			t.Error("WriteSession() should reject session from different agent")
   312→		}
   313→	})
   314→}
   315→
   316→// TestClaudeCodeHelperMethods verifies Claude-specific helper methods.
   317→func TestClaudeCodeHelperMethods(t *testing.T) {
   318→	t.Parallel()
   319→
   320→	t.Run("GetLastUserPrompt extracts last user message", func(t *testing.T) {
   321→		t.Parallel()
   322→		env := NewTestEnv(t)
   323→
   324→		transcriptPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, "transcript.jsonl")
   325→		content := `{"type":"user","uuid":"u1","message":{"content":"first prompt"}}
   326→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a1","message":{"content":[]}}
   327→{"type":"user","uuid":"u2","message":{"content":"second prompt"}}
   328→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a2","message":{"content":[]}}
   329→`
   330→		if err := os.WriteFile(transcriptPath, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {
   331→			t.Fatalf("failed to write transcript: %v", err)
   332→		}
   333→
   334→		ag, _ := agent.Get("claude-code")
   335→		ccAgent := ag.(*claudecode.ClaudeCodeAgent)
   336→
   337→		session, _ := ag.ReadSession(&agent.HookInput{
   338→			SessionID:  "test",
   339→			SessionRef: transcriptPath,
   340→		})
   341→
   342→		prompt := ccAgent.GetLastUserPrompt(session)
   343→		if prompt != "second prompt" {
   344→			t.Errorf("GetLastUserPrompt() = %q, want %q", prompt, "second prompt")
   345→		}
   346→	})
   347→
   348→	t.Run("TruncateAtUUID truncates transcript", func(t *testing.T) {
   349→		t.Parallel()
   350→		env := NewTestEnv(t)
   351→
   352→		transcriptPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, "transcript.jsonl")
   353→		content := `{"type":"user","uuid":"u1","message":{"content":"first"}}
   354→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a1","message":{"content":[]}}
   355→{"type":"user","uuid":"u2","message":{"content":"second"}}
   356→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a2","message":{"content":[]}}
   357→`
   358→		if err := os.WriteFile(transcriptPath, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {
   359→			t.Fatalf("failed to write transcript: %v", err)
   360→		}
   361→
   362→		ag, _ := agent.Get("claude-code")
   363→		ccAgent := ag.(*claudecode.ClaudeCodeAgent)
   364→
   365→		session, _ := ag.ReadSession(&agent.HookInput{
   366→			SessionID:  "test",
   367→			SessionRef: transcriptPath,
   368→		})
   369→
   370→		truncated, err := ccAgent.TruncateAtUUID(session, "a1")
   371→		if err != nil {
   372→			t.Fatalf("TruncateAtUUID() error = %v", err)
   373→		}
   374→
   375→		// Parse the truncated native data to verify
   376→		lines, _ := transcript.ParseFromBytes(truncated.NativeData)
   377→		if len(lines) != 2 {
   378→			t.Errorf("truncated transcript has %d lines, want 2", len(lines))
   379→		}
   380→		if lines[len(lines)-1].UUID != "a1" {
   381→			t.Errorf("last line UUID = %q, want %q", lines[len(lines)-1].UUID, "a1")
   382→		}
   383→	})
   384→
   385→	t.Run("FindCheckpointUUID finds tool result", func(t *testing.T) {
   386→		t.Parallel()
   387→		env := NewTestEnv(t)
   388→
   389→		transcriptPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, "transcript.jsonl")
   390→		content := `{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a1","message":{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"tool-123"}]}}
   391→{"type":"user","uuid":"u1","message":{"content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"tool-123"}]}}
   392→`
   393→		if err := os.WriteFile(transcriptPath, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {
   394→			t.Fatalf("failed to write transcript: %v", err)
   395→		}
   396→
   397→		ag, _ := agent.Get("claude-code")
   398→		ccAgent := ag.(*claudecode.ClaudeCodeAgent)

   401→			SessionID:  "test",
   402→			SessionRef: transcriptPath,
   403→		})
   404→
   405→		uuid, found := ccAgent.FindCheckpointUUID(session, "tool-123")
   406→		if !found {
   407→			t.Error("FindCheckpointUUID() found = false, want true")
   408→		}
   409→		if uuid != "u1" {
   410→			t.Errorf("FindCheckpointUUID() uuid = %q, want %q", uuid, "u1")
   411→		}
   412→	})
   413→
   414→}

   416→// TestGeminiCLIAgentDetection verifies Gemini CLI agent detection.
   417→// Not parallel - contains subtests that use os.Chdir which is process-global.
   418→func TestGeminiCLIAgentDetection(t *testing.T) {
   419→
   420→	t.Run("gemini agent is registered", func(t *testing.T) {
   421→		t.Parallel()
   422→
   423→		agents := agent.List()
   424→		found := false
   425→		for _, name := range agents {
   426→			if name == "gemini" {
   427→				found = true
   428→				break
   429→			}
   430→		}
   431→		if !found {
   432→			t.Errorf("agent.List() = %v, want to contain 'gemini'", agents)
   433→		}
   434→	})
   435→
   436→	t.Run("gemini detects presence when .gemini exists", func(t *testing.T) {
   437→		// Not parallel - uses os.Chdir which is process-global
   438→		env := NewTestEnv(t)
   439→		env.InitRepo()
   440→
   441→		// Create .gemini/settings.json
   442→		geminiDir := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini")
   443→		if err := os.MkdirAll(geminiDir, 0o755); err != nil {
   444→			t.Fatalf("failed to create .gemini dir: %v", err)
   445→		}
   446→		settingsPath := filepath.Join(geminiDir, geminicli.GeminiSettingsFileName)
   447→		if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, []byte(`{"hooks":{}}`), 0o644); err != nil {
   448→			t.Fatalf("failed to write settings.json: %v", err)
   449→		}
   450→
   451→		// Change to repo dir for detection
   452→		oldWd, _ := os.Getwd()
   453→		if err := os.Chdir(env.RepoDir); err != nil {
   454→			t.Fatalf("failed to chdir: %v", err)
   455→		}
   456→		defer func() { _ = os.Chdir(oldWd) }()
   457→
   458→		ag, err := agent.Get("gemini")
   459→		if err != nil {
   460→			t.Fatalf("Get(gemini) error = %v", err)
   461→		}
   462→
   463→		present, err := ag.DetectPresence()
   464→		if err != nil {
   465→			t.Fatalf("DetectPresence() error = %v", err)
   466→		}
   467→		if !present {
   468→			t.Error("DetectPresence() = false, want true when .gemini exists")
   469→		}
   470→	})
   471→}

   473→// TestGeminiCLIHookInstallation verifies hook installation via Gemini CLI agent interface.
   474→// Note: These tests cannot run in parallel because they use os.Chdir which affects the entire process.
   475→func TestGeminiCLIHookInstallation(t *testing.T) {
   476→	// Not parallel - tests use os.Chdir which is process-global
   477→
   478→	t.Run("installs all required hooks", func(t *testing.T) {
   479→		// Not parallel - uses os.Chdir
   480→		env := NewTestEnv(t)
   481→		env.InitRepo()
   482→
   483→		// Change to repo dir
   484→		oldWd, _ := os.Getwd()
   485→		if err := os.Chdir(env.RepoDir); err != nil {
   486→			t.Fatalf("failed to chdir: %v", err)
   487→		}
   488→		defer func() { _ = os.Chdir(oldWd) }()
   489→
   490→		ag, err := agent.Get("gemini")
   491→		if err != nil {
   492→			t.Fatalf("Get(gemini) error = %v", err)
   493→		}
   494→
   495→		hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
   496→		if !ok {
   497→			t.Fatal("gemini agent does not implement HookSupport")
   498→		}
   499→
   500→		count, err := hookAgent.InstallHooks(false, false)
   501→		if err != nil {
   502→			t.Fatalf("InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
   503→		}
   504→
   505→		// Should install 12 hooks: SessionStart, SessionEnd (exit+logout), BeforeAgent, AfterAgent,
   506→		// BeforeModel, AfterModel, BeforeToolSelection, BeforeTool, AfterTool, PreCompress, Notification
   507→		if count != 12 {
   508→			t.Errorf("InstallHooks() count = %d, want 12", count)
   509→		}
   510→
   511→		// Verify hooks are installed
   512→		if !hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled() {
   513→			t.Error("AreHooksInstalled() = false after InstallHooks()")
   514→		}
   515→
   516→		// Verify settings.json was created
   517→		settingsPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini", geminicli.GeminiSettingsFileName)
   518→		if _, err := os.Stat(settingsPath); os.IsNotExist(err) {
   519→			t.Error("settings.json was not created")
   520→		}
   521→
   522→		// Verify hooks structure in settings.json
   523→		data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath)
   524→		if err != nil {
   525→			t.Fatalf("failed to read settings.json: %v", err)
   526→		}
   527→		content := string(data)
   528→
   529→		// Verify all hook types are present
   530→		if !strings.Contains(content, "SessionStart") {
   531→			t.Error("settings.json should contain SessionStart hook")
   532→		}
   533→		if !strings.Contains(content, "SessionEnd") {
   534→			t.Error("settings.json should contain SessionEnd hook")
   535→		}
   536→		if !strings.Contains(content, "BeforeAgent") {
   537→			t.Error("settings.json should contain BeforeAgent hook")
   538→		}
   539→		if !strings.Contains(content, "AfterAgent") {
   540→			t.Error("settings.json should contain AfterAgent hook")
   541→		}
   542→		if !strings.Contains(content, "BeforeModel") {
   543→			t.Error("settings.json should contain BeforeModel hook")
   544→		}
   545→		if !strings.Contains(content, "AfterModel") {
   546→			t.Error("settings.json should contain AfterModel hook")
   547→		}
   548→		if !strings.Contains(content, "BeforeToolSelection") {
   549→			t.Error("settings.json should contain BeforeToolSelection hook")
   550→		}
   551→		if !strings.Contains(content, "BeforeTool") {
   552→			t.Error("settings.json should contain BeforeTool hook")
   553→		}
   554→		if !strings.Contains(content, "AfterTool") {
   555→			t.Error("settings.json should contain AfterTool hook")
   556→		}
   557→		if !strings.Contains(content, "PreCompress") {
   558→			t.Error("settings.json should contain PreCompress hook")
   559→		}
   560→		if !strings.Contains(content, "Notification") {
   561→			t.Error("settings.json should contain Notification hook")
   562→		}
   563→
   564→		// Verify hooksConfig is set
   565→		if !strings.Contains(content, "hooksConfig") {
   566→			t.Error("settings.json should contain hooksConfig.enabled")
   567→		}
   568→	})
   569→
   570→	t.Run("idempotent - second install returns 0", func(t *testing.T) {
   571→		// Not parallel - uses os.Chdir
   572→		env := NewTestEnv(t)
   573→		env.InitRepo()
   574→
   575→		oldWd, _ := os.Getwd()
   576→		if err := os.Chdir(env.RepoDir); err != nil {
   577→			t.Fatalf("failed to chdir: %v", err)
   578→		}
   579→		defer func() { _ = os.Chdir(oldWd) }()
   580→
   581→		ag, _ := agent.Get("gemini")
   582→		hookAgent := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
   583→
   584→		// First install
   585→		_, err := hookAgent.InstallHooks(false, false)
   586→		if err != nil {
   587→			t.Fatalf("first InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
   588→		}
   589→
   590→		// Second install should be idempotent
   591→		count, err := hookAgent.InstallHooks(false, false)
   592→		if err != nil {
   593→			t.Fatalf("second InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
   594→		}
   595→		if count != 0 {
   596→			t.Errorf("second InstallHooks() count = %d, want 0 (idempotent)", count)
   597→		}
   598→	})
   599→
   600→	t.Run("localDev mode uses go run", func(t *testing.T) {
   601→		// Not parallel - uses os.Chdir
   602→		env := NewTestEnv(t)
   603→		env.InitRepo()
   604→
   605→		oldWd, _ := os.Getwd()
   606→		if err := os.Chdir(env.RepoDir); err != nil {
   607→			t.Fatalf("failed to chdir: %v", err)
   608→		}
   609→		defer func() { _ = os.Chdir(oldWd) }()
   610→
   611→		ag, _ := agent.Get("gemini")
   612→		hookAgent := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
   613→
   614→		_, err := hookAgent.InstallHooks(true, false) // localDev = true
   615→		if err != nil {
   616→			t.Fatalf("InstallHooks(localDev=true) error = %v", err)
   617→		}
   618→
   619→		// Read settings and verify commands use "go run"
   620→		settingsPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini", geminicli.GeminiSettingsFileName)
   621→		data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath)
   622→		if err != nil {
   623→			t.Fatalf("failed to read settings.json: %v", err)
   624→		}
   625→
   626→		content := string(data)
   627→		if !strings.Contains(content, "go run") {
   628→			t.Error("localDev hooks should use 'go run', but settings.json doesn't contain it")
   629→		}
   630→		if !strings.Contains(content, "${GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR}") {
   631→			t.Error("localDev hooks should use '${GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR}', but settings.json doesn't contain it")
   632→		}
   633→	})
   634→
   635→	t.Run("production mode uses entire binary", func(t *testing.T) {
   636→		// Not parallel - uses os.Chdir
   637→		env := NewTestEnv(t)
   638→		env.InitRepo()
   639→
   640→		oldWd, _ := os.Getwd()
   641→		if err := os.Chdir(env.RepoDir); err != nil {
   642→			t.Fatalf("failed to chdir: %v", err)
   643→		}
   644→		defer func() { _ = os.Chdir(oldWd) }()
   645→
   646→		ag, _ := agent.Get("gemini")
   647→		hookAgent := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
   648→
   649→		_, err := hookAgent.InstallHooks(false, false) // localDev = false
   650→		if err != nil {
   651→			t.Fatalf("InstallHooks(localDev=false) error = %v", err)
   652→		}
   653→
   654→		// Read settings and verify commands use "entire" binary
   655→		settingsPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini", geminicli.GeminiSettingsFileName)
   656→		data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath)
   657→		if err != nil {
   658→			t.Fatalf("failed to read settings.json: %v", err)
   659→		}
   660→
   661→		content := string(data)
   662→		if !strings.Contains(content, "entire hooks gemini") {
   663→			t.Error("production hooks should use 'entire hooks gemini', but settings.json doesn't contain it")
   664→		}
   665→	})
   666→
   667→	t.Run("force flag reinstalls hooks", func(t *testing.T) {
   668→		// Not parallel - uses os.Chdir
   669→		env := NewTestEnv(t)
   670→		env.InitRepo()
   671→
   672→		oldWd, _ := os.Getwd()
   673→		if err := os.Chdir(env.RepoDir); err != nil {
   674→			t.Fatalf("failed to chdir: %v", err)
   675→		}
   676→		defer func() { _ = os.Chdir(oldWd) }()
   677→
   678→		ag, _ := agent.Get("gemini")
   679→		hookAgent := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
   680→
   681→		// First install
   682→		_, err := hookAgent.InstallHooks(false, false)
   683→		if err != nil {
   684→			t.Fatalf("first InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
   685→		}
   686→
   687→		// Force reinstall should return count > 0
   688→		count, err := hookAgent.InstallHooks(false, true) // force = true
   689→		if err != nil {
   690→			t.Fatalf("force InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
   691→		}
   692→		if count != 12 {
   693→			t.Errorf("force InstallHooks() count = %d, want 12", count)
   694→		}
   695→	})
   696→}
   697→
   698→// TestGeminiCLISessionOperations verifies ReadSession/WriteSession via Gemini agent interface.
   699→func TestGeminiCLISessionOperations(t *testing.T) {
   700→	t.Parallel()
   701→
   702→	t.Run("ReadSession parses transcript and computes ModifiedFiles", func(t *testing.T) {
   703→		t.Parallel()
   704→		env := NewTestEnv(t)
   705→		env.InitRepo()
   706→
   707→		// Create a Gemini transcript file (JSON format)
   708→		// Gemini uses "type" field with values "user" or "gemini", and "toolCalls" array with "args"
   709→		transcriptPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, "test-transcript.json")
   710→		transcriptContent := `{
   711→  "messages": [
   712→    {"type": "user", "content": "Fix the bug"},
   713→    {"type": "gemini", "content": "", "toolCalls": [{"name": "write_file", "args": {"file_path": "main.go"}}]},
   714→    {"type": "gemini", "content": "", "toolCalls": [{"name": "edit_file", "args": {"file_path": "util.go"}}]}
   715→  ]
   716→}`
   717→		if err := os.WriteFile(transcriptPath, []byte(transcriptContent), 0o644); err != nil {
   718→			t.Fatalf("failed to write transcript: %v", err)
   719→		}
   720→
   721→		ag, _ := agent.Get("gemini")
   722→		session, err := ag.ReadSession(&agent.HookInput{
   723→			SessionID:  "test-session",
   724→			SessionRef: transcriptPath,
   725→		})
   726→		if err != nil {
   727→			t.Fatalf("ReadSession() error = %v", err)
   728→		}
   729→

   106→// TestAgentTranscriptRestoration verifies transcript is restored correctly on rewind.
   107→func TestAgentTranscriptRestoration(t *testing.T) {
   108→	t.Parallel()
   109→
   110→	// Only test with manual-commit strategy as it has full transcript restoration
   111→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   112→
   113→	ag, _ := agent.Get("claude-code")
   114→
   115→	// Create first session
   116→	session1 := env.NewSession()
   117→	env.WriteFile("file1.go", "package main\n// file1 v1")
   118→	transcript1 := session1.CreateTranscript("Create file1", []FileChange{
   119→		{Path: "file1.go", Content: "package main\n// file1 v1"},
   120→	})
   121→
   122→	env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session1.ID)
   123→	env.SimulateStop(session1.ID, transcript1)
   124→
   125→	// Get checkpoint after first prompt
   126→	points1 := env.GetRewindPoints()
   127→	if len(points1) == 0 {
   128→		t.Fatal("no rewind point after first prompt")
   129→	}
   130→	checkpoint1ID := points1[0].ID
   131→
   132→	// Continue the SAME session with second prompt (manual-commit strategy requires same session on same base commit)
   133→	// Reset transcript builder for the new checkpoint
   134→	session1.TranscriptBuilder = NewTranscriptBuilder()
   135→	env.WriteFile("file1.go", "package main\n// file1 v2")
   136→	env.WriteFile("file2.go", "package main\n// file2")
   137→	transcript2 := session1.CreateTranscript("Modify file1, create file2", []FileChange{
   138→		{Path: "file1.go", Content: "package main\n// file1 v2"},
   139→		{Path: "file2.go", Content: "package main\n// file2"},
   140→	})
   141→
   142→	env.SimulateUserPromptSubmit(session1.ID)
   143→	env.SimulateStop(session1.ID, transcript2)
   144→
   145→	// Verify we have 2 checkpoints
   146→	points2 := env.GetRewindPoints()
   147→	if len(points2) < 2 {

   149→	}

   151→	// Rewind to first checkpoint

   154→	}
   155→
   156→	// Verify file content is restored
   157→	content := env.ReadFile("file1.go")
   158→	if content != "package main\n// file1 v1" {
   159→		t.Errorf("file1.go content after rewind = %q, want v1 content", content)
   160→	}
   161→
   162→	// file2.go should not exist after rewind to checkpoint 1

   172→	}

   313→
   314→// stringReader implements io.Reader for test strings
   315→type stringReader struct {
   316→	data []byte
   317→	pos  int
   318→}
   319→
   320→func newStringReader(s string) *stringReader {
   321→	return &stringReader{data: []byte(s)}
   322→}
   323→
   324→func (r *stringReader) Read(p []byte) (n int, err error) {
   325→	if r.pos >= len(r.data) {
   326→		return 0, io.EOF
   327→	}
   328→	n = copy(p, r.data[r.pos:])
   329→	r.pos += n
   330→	return n, nil
   331→}

   359→	if !strings.Contains(string(data), `"agent"`) && !strings.Contains(string(data), `"agent":`) {
   360→		t.Logf("settings content: %s", data)
   361→		// Agent field may be omitted if default
   362→	}

    16→
    17→// TestSetupGeminiHooks_AddsAllRequiredHooks is a smoke test verifying that
    18→// `entire enable --agent gemini` adds all required hooks to the correct file.
    19→func TestSetupGeminiHooks_AddsAllRequiredHooks(t *testing.T) {
    20→	t.Parallel()
    21→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
    22→	env.InitRepo()

    24→

    28→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
    29→
    30→	// Run entire enable --agent gemini (non-interactive)
    31→	output, err := env.RunCLIWithError("enable", "--agent", "gemini")
    32→	if err != nil {
    33→		t.Fatalf("enable gemini command failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)

    35→

    37→	settings := readGeminiSettingsFile(t, env)
    38→

    41→		t.Error("hooksConfig.enabled should be true")
    42→	}
    43→
    44→	// Verify all hooks exist (12 total, but SessionEnd has 2 matchers)
    45→	if len(settings.Hooks.SessionStart) == 0 {
    46→		t.Error("SessionStart hook should exist")
    47→	}
    48→	if len(settings.Hooks.SessionEnd) < 2 {
    49→		t.Errorf("SessionEnd hooks should have 2 matchers (exit + logout), got %d", len(settings.Hooks.SessionEnd))
    50→	}
    51→	if len(settings.Hooks.BeforeAgent) == 0 {

    53→	}
    54→	if len(settings.Hooks.AfterAgent) == 0 {
    55→		t.Error("AfterAgent hook should exist")
    56→	}
    57→	if len(settings.Hooks.BeforeModel) == 0 {

    60→	if len(settings.Hooks.AfterModel) == 0 {

    74→	}
    75→	if len(settings.Hooks.Notification) == 0 {
    76→		t.Error("Notification hook should exist")
    77→	}
    78→}
    79→
    80→// TestSetupGeminiHooks_PreservesExistingSettings is a smoke test verifying that
    81→// enable gemini doesn't nuke existing settings or user-configured hooks.
    82→func TestSetupGeminiHooks_PreservesExistingSettings(t *testing.T) {
    83→	t.Parallel()
    84→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
    85→	env.InitRepo()
    86→	env.InitEntire("manual-commit")
    87→
    88→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test")
    89→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
    90→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
    91→
    92→	// Create existing settings with custom fields and user hooks
    93→	geminiDir := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini")
    94→	if err := os.MkdirAll(geminiDir, 0o755); err != nil {
    95→		t.Fatalf("failed to create .gemini dir: %v", err)
    96→	}
    97→
    98→	existingSettings := `{
    99→  "customSetting": "should-be-preserved",
   100→  "hooks": {
   101→    "SessionStart": [
   102→      {
   103→        "matcher": "startup",
   104→        "hooks": [{"name": "my-hook", "type": "command", "command": "echo user-startup-hook"}]
   105→      }
   106→    ]
   107→  }
   108→}`
   109→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(geminiDir, geminicli.GeminiSettingsFileName)
   110→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, []byte(existingSettings), 0o644); err != nil {
   111→		t.Fatalf("failed to write existing settings: %v", err)
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	// Run enable gemini
   115→	output, err := env.RunCLIWithError("enable", "--agent", "gemini")
   116→	if err != nil {
   117→		t.Fatalf("enable gemini failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
   118→	}
   119→
   120→	// Verify custom setting is preserved
   121→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath)
   122→	if err != nil {
   123→		t.Fatalf("failed to read settings.json: %v", err)
   124→	}
   125→
   126→	var rawSettings map[string]interface{}
   127→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &rawSettings); err != nil {
   128→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse settings.json: %v", err)
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	if rawSettings["customSetting"] != "should-be-preserved" {
   132→		t.Error("customSetting should be preserved after enable gemini")
   133→	}
   134→
   135→	// Verify user hooks are preserved
   136→	settings := readGeminiSettingsFile(t, env)
   137→
   138→	// User's startup matcher hook should still exist
   139→	foundUserHook := false
   140→	for _, matcher := range settings.Hooks.SessionStart {
   141→		if matcher.Matcher == "startup" {
   142→			for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   143→				if hook.Name == "my-hook" && hook.Command == "echo user-startup-hook" {
   144→					foundUserHook = true
   145→				}
   146→			}
   147→		}
   148→	}
   149→	if !foundUserHook {
   150→		t.Error("existing user hook 'my-hook' should be preserved")
   151→	}
   152→
   153→	// Our hooks should also be added
   154→	if len(settings.Hooks.AfterAgent) == 0 {
   155→		t.Error("AfterAgent hook should be added")
   156→	}
   157→	if len(settings.Hooks.BeforeAgent) == 0 {
   158→		t.Error("BeforeAgent hook should be added")
   159→	}
   160→}
   161→
   162→// Helper functions
   163→
   164→func readGeminiSettingsFile(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv) GeminiSettings {
   165→	t.Helper()
   166→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini", geminicli.GeminiSettingsFileName)
   167→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath)
   168→	if err != nil {
   169→		t.Fatalf("failed to read %s at %s: %v", geminicli.GeminiSettingsFileName, settingsPath, err)
   170→	}
   171→
   172→	var settings GeminiSettings
   173→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {
   174→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse settings.json: %v", err)
   175→	}
   176→	return settings
   177→}
   178→

    64→// InstallHooks installs Factory AI Droid hooks in .factory/settings.json.
    65→// If force is true, removes existing Entire hooks before installing.
    66→// Returns the number of hooks installed.
    67→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error) {
    68→	// Use repo root instead of CWD to find .factory directory
    69→	// This ensures hooks are installed correctly when run from a subdirectory

   141→		sessionEndCmd = "go run ${FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks factoryai-droid session-end"

   174→		count++
   175→	}
   176→	if !hookCommandExistsWithMatcher(preToolUse, "Task", preTaskCmd) {
   177→		preToolUse = addHookToMatcher(preToolUse, "Task", preTaskCmd)
   178→		count++
   179→	}
   180→	if !hookCommandExistsWithMatcher(postToolUse, "Task", postTaskCmd) {
   181→		postToolUse = addHookToMatcher(postToolUse, "Task", postTaskCmd)
   182→		count++
   183→	}
   184→	if !hookCommandExists(preCompact, preCompactCmd) {
   185→		preCompact = addHookToMatcher(preCompact, "", preCompactCmd)
   186→		count++
   187→	}
   188→
   189→	// Add permissions.deny rule if not present
   190→	permissionsChanged := false
   191→	var denyRules []string
   192→	if denyRaw, ok := rawPermissions["deny"]; ok {
   193→		if err := json.Unmarshal(denyRaw, &denyRules); err != nil {
   194→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse permissions.deny in settings.json: %w", err)
   195→		}
   196→	}
   197→	if !slices.Contains(denyRules, metadataDenyRule) {
   198→		denyRules = append(denyRules, metadataDenyRule)
   199→		denyJSON, err := json.Marshal(denyRules)
   200→		if err != nil {
   201→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal permissions.deny: %w", err)
   202→		}
   203→		rawPermissions["deny"] = denyJSON
   204→		permissionsChanged = true
   205→	}
   206→
   207→	if count == 0 && !permissionsChanged {
   208→		return 0, nil // All hooks and permissions already installed
   209→	}
   210→
   211→	// Marshal modified hook types back to rawHooks
   212→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "SessionStart", sessionStart)
   213→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", sessionEnd)
   214→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "Stop", stop)
   215→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "UserPromptSubmit", userPromptSubmit)
   216→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "PreToolUse", preToolUse)
   217→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "PostToolUse", postToolUse)
   218→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "PreCompact", preCompact)
   219→
   220→	// Marshal hooks and update raw settings
   221→	hooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawHooks)
   222→	if err != nil {
   223→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hooks: %w", err)
   224→	}
   225→	rawSettings["hooks"] = hooksJSON
   226→
   227→	// Marshal permissions and update raw settings
   228→	permJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawPermissions)
   229→	if err != nil {
   230→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal permissions: %w", err)
   231→	}
   232→	rawSettings["permissions"] = permJSON
   233→
   234→	// Write back to file
   235→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(settingsPath), 0o750); err != nil {
   236→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create .factory directory: %w", err)
   237→	}
   238→
   239→	output, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(rawSettings, "", "  ")
   240→	if err != nil {
   241→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal settings: %w", err)
   242→	}
   243→
   244→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   245→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to write settings.json: %w", err)
   246→	}
   247→
   248→	return count, nil
   249→}
   250→
   251→// parseHookType parses a specific hook type from rawHooks into the target slice.
   252→// Silently ignores parse errors (leaves target unchanged).
   253→func parseHookType(rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage, hookType string, target *[]FactoryHookMatcher) {
   254→	if data, ok := rawHooks[hookType]; ok {
   255→		//nolint:errcheck,gosec // Intentionally ignoring parse errors - leave target as nil/empty
   256→		json.Unmarshal(data, target)
   257→	}
   258→}
   259→
   260→// marshalHookType marshals a hook type back to rawHooks.
   261→// If the slice is empty, removes the key from rawHooks.
   262→func marshalHookType(rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage, hookType string, matchers []FactoryHookMatcher) {
   263→	if len(matchers) == 0 {
   264→		delete(rawHooks, hookType)
   265→		return
   266→	}
   267→	data, err := json.Marshal(matchers)
   268→	if err != nil {
   269→		return // Silently ignore marshal errors (shouldn't happen)
   270→	}
   271→	rawHooks[hookType] = data
   272→}
   273→
   274→// UninstallHooks removes Entire hooks from Factory AI Droid settings.
   275→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) UninstallHooks() error {
   276→	// Use repo root to find .factory directory when run from a subdirectory
   277→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   278→	if err != nil {
   279→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   280→	}
   281→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".factory", FactorySettingsFileName)
   282→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   283→	if err != nil {
   284→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // No settings file means nothing to uninstall
   285→	}
   286→
   287→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
   288→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &rawSettings); err != nil {
   289→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse settings.json: %w", err)
   290→	}
   291→
   292→	// rawHooks preserves unknown hook types
   293→	var rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage
   294→	if hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings["hooks"]; ok {
   295→		if err := json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &rawHooks); err != nil {
   296→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hooks: %w", err)
   297→		}
   298→	}
   299→	if rawHooks == nil {
   300→		rawHooks = make(map[string]json.RawMessage)
   301→	}
   302→
   303→	// Parse only the hook types we need to modify
   304→	var sessionStart, sessionEnd, stop, userPromptSubmit, preToolUse, postToolUse, preCompact []FactoryHookMatcher
   305→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "SessionStart", &sessionStart)
   306→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", &sessionEnd)
   307→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "Stop", &stop)
   308→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "UserPromptSubmit", &userPromptSubmit)
   309→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "PreToolUse", &preToolUse)
   310→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "PostToolUse", &postToolUse)
   311→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "PreCompact", &preCompact)
   312→
   313→	// Remove Entire hooks from all hook types
   314→	sessionStart = removeEntireHooks(sessionStart)
   315→	sessionEnd = removeEntireHooks(sessionEnd)
   316→	stop = removeEntireHooks(stop)
   317→	userPromptSubmit = removeEntireHooks(userPromptSubmit)
   318→	preToolUse = removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(preToolUse)
   319→	postToolUse = removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(postToolUse)
   320→	preCompact = removeEntireHooks(preCompact)
   321→
   322→	// Marshal modified hook types back to rawHooks
   323→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "SessionStart", sessionStart)
   324→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", sessionEnd)
   325→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "Stop", stop)
   326→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "UserPromptSubmit", userPromptSubmit)
   327→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "PreToolUse", preToolUse)
   328→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "PostToolUse", postToolUse)
   329→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "PreCompact", preCompact)
   330→
   331→	// Also remove the metadata deny rule from permissions
   332→	var rawPermissions map[string]json.RawMessage
   333→	if permRaw, ok := rawSettings["permissions"]; ok {
   334→		if err := json.Unmarshal(permRaw, &rawPermissions); err != nil {
   335→			// If parsing fails, just skip permissions cleanup
   336→			rawPermissions = nil
   337→		}
   338→	}
   339→
   340→	if rawPermissions != nil {
   341→		if denyRaw, ok := rawPermissions["deny"]; ok {
   342→			var denyRules []string
   343→			if err := json.Unmarshal(denyRaw, &denyRules); err == nil {
   344→				// Filter out the metadata deny rule
   345→				filteredRules := make([]string, 0, len(denyRules))
   346→				for _, rule := range denyRules {
   347→					if rule != metadataDenyRule {
   348→						filteredRules = append(filteredRules, rule)
   349→					}
   350→				}
   351→				if len(filteredRules) > 0 {
   352→					denyJSON, err := json.Marshal(filteredRules)
   353→					if err == nil {
   354→						rawPermissions["deny"] = denyJSON
   355→					}
   356→				} else {
   357→					// Remove empty deny array
   358→					delete(rawPermissions, "deny")
   359→				}
   360→			}
   361→		}
   362→
   363→		// If permissions is empty, remove it entirely
   364→		if len(rawPermissions) > 0 {
   365→			permJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawPermissions)
   366→			if err == nil {
   367→				rawSettings["permissions"] = permJSON
   368→			}
   369→		} else {
   370→			delete(rawSettings, "permissions")
   371→		}
   372→	}
   373→
   374→	// Marshal hooks back (preserving unknown hook types)
   375→	if len(rawHooks) > 0 {
   376→		hooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawHooks)
   377→		if err != nil {
   378→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hooks: %w", err)
   379→		}
   380→		rawSettings["hooks"] = hooksJSON
   381→	} else {
   382→		delete(rawSettings, "hooks")
   383→	}
   384→
   385→	// Write back
   386→	output, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(rawSettings, "", "  ")
   387→	if err != nil {
   388→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal settings: %w", err)
   389→	}
   390→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   391→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write settings.json: %w", err)
   392→	}
   393→	return nil
   394→}
   395→
   396→// AreHooksInstalled checks if Entire hooks are installed.
   397→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) AreHooksInstalled() bool {
   398→	// Use repo root to find .factory directory when run from a subdirectory
   399→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   400→	if err != nil {
   401→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   402→	}
   403→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".factory", FactorySettingsFileName)
   404→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   405→	if err != nil {
   406→		return false
   407→	}
   408→
   409→	var settings FactorySettings
   410→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {
   411→		return false
   412→	}
   413→
   414→	// Check for at least one of our hooks (new or old format)
   415→	return hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "entire hooks factoryai-droid stop") ||
   416→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "go run ${FACTORY_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks factoryai-droid stop")
   417→}
   418→
   419→// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types Factory AI Droid supports.
   420→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) GetSupportedHooks() []agent.HookType {
   421→	return []agent.HookType{
   422→		agent.HookSessionStart,
   423→		agent.HookSessionEnd,
   424→		agent.HookUserPromptSubmit,
   425→		agent.HookStop,
   426→		agent.HookPreToolUse,
   427→		agent.HookPostToolUse,
   428→	}
   429→}
   430→
   431→// Helper functions for hook management
   432→
   433→func hookCommandExists(matchers []FactoryHookMatcher, command string) bool {
   434→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   435→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   436→			if hook.Command == command {
   437→				return true
   438→			}
   439→		}
   440→	}
   441→	return false
   442→}
   443→
   444→func hookCommandExistsWithMatcher(matchers []FactoryHookMatcher, matcherName, command string) bool {
   445→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   446→		if matcher.Matcher == matcherName {
   447→			for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   448→				if hook.Command == command {
   449→					return true
   450→				}
   451→			}
   452→		}
   453→	}
   454→	return false
   455→}
   456→
   457→func addHookToMatcher(matchers []FactoryHookMatcher, matcherName, command string) []FactoryHookMatcher {
   458→	entry := FactoryHookEntry{
   459→		Type:    "command",
   460→		Command: command,
   461→	}
   462→
   463→	// If no matcher name, add to a matcher with empty string
   464→	if matcherName == "" {
   465→		for i, matcher := range matchers {
   466→			if matcher.Matcher == "" {
   467→				matchers[i].Hooks = append(matchers[i].Hooks, entry)
   468→				return matchers
   469→			}
   470→		}
   471→		return append(matchers, FactoryHookMatcher{
   472→			Matcher: "",
   473→			Hooks:   []FactoryHookEntry{entry},
   474→		})
   475→	}
   476→
   477→	// Find or create matcher with the given name
   478→	for i, matcher := range matchers {
   479→		if matcher.Matcher == matcherName {
   480→			matchers[i].Hooks = append(matchers[i].Hooks, entry)
   481→			return matchers
   482→		}
   483→	}
   484→
   485→	return append(matchers, FactoryHookMatcher{
   486→		Matcher: matcherName,
   487→		Hooks:   []FactoryHookEntry{entry},
   488→	})
   489→}
   490→
   491→// isEntireHook checks if a command is an Entire hook (old or new format)
   492→func isEntireHook(command string) bool {
   493→	for _, prefix := range entireHookPrefixes {
   494→		if strings.HasPrefix(command, prefix) {
   495→			return true
   496→		}
   497→	}
   498→	return false
   499→}
   500→
   501→// removeEntireHooks removes all Entire hooks from a list of matchers (for simple hooks like Stop)
   502→func removeEntireHooks(matchers []FactoryHookMatcher) []FactoryHookMatcher {
   503→	result := make([]FactoryHookMatcher, 0, len(matchers))
   504→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   505→		filteredHooks := make([]FactoryHookEntry, 0, len(matcher.Hooks))
   506→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   507→			if !isEntireHook(hook.Command) {
   508→				filteredHooks = append(filteredHooks, hook)
   509→			}
   510→		}
   511→		// Only keep the matcher if it has hooks remaining
   512→		if len(filteredHooks) > 0 {
   513→			matcher.Hooks = filteredHooks
   514→			result = append(result, matcher)
   515→		}
   516→	}
   517→	return result
   518→}
   519→
   520→// removeEntireHooksFromMatchers removes Entire hooks from tool-use matchers (PreToolUse, PostToolUse)
   521→// This handles the nested structure where hooks are grouped by tool matcher (e.g., "Task")
   522→func removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(matchers []FactoryHookMatcher) []FactoryHookMatcher {
   523→	// Same logic as removeEntireHooks - both work on the same structure
   524→	return removeEntireHooks(matchers)
   525→}

     1→package factoryaidroid
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"bufio"
     5→	"bytes"
     6→	"encoding/json"
     7→	"fmt"
     8→	"io"
     9→	"os"
    10→	"path/filepath"
    11→	"slices"
    12→	"strings"
    13→
    14→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    15→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript"
    16→)
    17→
    18→// TranscriptLine is an alias to the shared transcript.Line type.
    19→type TranscriptLine = transcript.Line
    20→
    21→// droidEnvelope is the top-level structure of a Factory AI Droid JSONL line.
    22→// Droid wraps messages as {"type":"message","id":"...","message":{"role":"assistant","content":[...]}},
    23→// unlike Claude Code which uses {"type":"assistant","uuid":"...","message":{"content":[...]}}.
    24→type droidEnvelope struct {
    25→	Type    string          `json:"type"`
    26→	ID      string          `json:"id"`
    27→	Message json.RawMessage `json:"message"`
    28→}
    29→
    30→// droidMessageRole extracts just the role from the inner message.
    31→type droidMessageRole struct {
    32→	Role string `json:"role"`
    33→}
    34→
    35→// ParseDroidTranscript parses a Droid JSONL file into normalized transcript.Line entries.
    36→// It transforms the Droid envelope format (type="message", role inside message) into the
    37→// shared transcript.Line format (type="assistant"/"user", message=inner content).
    38→// Non-message entries (session_start, etc.) are skipped.
    39→func ParseDroidTranscript(path string, startLine int) ([]transcript.Line, int, error) {
    40→	file, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path is a controlled transcript file path
    41→	if err != nil {
    42→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript: %w", err)
    43→	}
    44→	defer func() { _ = file.Close() }()
    45→
    46→	return parseDroidTranscriptFromReader(file, startLine)
    47→}
    48→
    49→// ParseDroidTranscriptFromBytes parses Droid JSONL content from a byte slice.
    50→func ParseDroidTranscriptFromBytes(content []byte) ([]transcript.Line, error) {
    51→	lines, _, err := parseDroidTranscriptFromReader(bytes.NewReader(content), 0)
    52→	return lines, err
    53→}
    54→
    55→func parseDroidTranscriptFromReader(r io.Reader, startLine int) ([]transcript.Line, int, error) {
    56→	reader := bufio.NewReader(r)
    57→	var lines []transcript.Line
    58→	totalLines := 0
    59→
    60→	for {
    61→		lineBytes, err := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
    62→		if err != nil && err != io.EOF {
    63→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    64→		}
    65→
    66→		if len(lineBytes) == 0 {
    67→			if err == io.EOF {
    68→				break
    69→			}
    70→			continue
    71→		}
    72→
    73→		if totalLines >= startLine {
    74→			if line, ok := parseDroidLine(lineBytes); ok {
    75→				lines = append(lines, line)
    76→			}
    77→		}
    78→		totalLines++
    79→
    80→		if err == io.EOF {
    81→			break
    82→		}
    83→	}
    84→
    85→	return lines, totalLines, nil
    86→}
    87→
    88→// parseDroidLine converts a single Droid JSONL line into a normalized transcript.Line.
    89→// Returns false if the line is not a message entry (e.g., session_start).
    90→func parseDroidLine(lineBytes []byte) (transcript.Line, bool) {
    91→	var env droidEnvelope
    92→	if err := json.Unmarshal(lineBytes, &env); err != nil {
    93→		return transcript.Line{}, false
    94→	}
    95→
    96→	// Only process "message" type entries — skip session_start, etc.
    97→	if env.Type != "message" {
    98→		return transcript.Line{}, false
    99→	}
   100→
   101→	// Extract role from the inner message
   102→	var role droidMessageRole
   103→	if err := json.Unmarshal(env.Message, &role); err != nil {
   104→		return transcript.Line{}, false
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	return transcript.Line{
   108→		Type:    role.Role, // "assistant" or "user"
   109→		UUID:    env.ID,
   110→		Message: env.Message,
   111→	}, true
   112→}
   113→
   114→// SerializeTranscript converts transcript lines back to JSONL bytes.
   115→func SerializeTranscript(lines []TranscriptLine) ([]byte, error) {
   116→	var buf bytes.Buffer
   117→	for _, line := range lines {
   118→		data, err := json.Marshal(line)
   119→		if err != nil {
   120→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal line: %w", err)
   121→		}
   122→		buf.Write(data)
   123→		buf.WriteByte('\n')
   124→	}
   125→	return buf.Bytes(), nil
   126→}
   127→
   128→// ExtractModifiedFiles extracts files modified by tool calls from transcript.
   129→func ExtractModifiedFiles(lines []TranscriptLine) []string {
   130→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
   131→	var files []string
   132→
   133→	for _, line := range lines {
   134→		if line.Type != "assistant" {
   135→			continue
   136→		}
   137→
   138→		var msg transcript.AssistantMessage
   139→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   140→			continue
   141→		}
   142→
   143→		for _, block := range msg.Content {
   144→			if block.Type != "tool_use" || !slices.Contains(FileModificationTools, block.Name) {
   145→				continue
   146→			}
   147→
   148→			var input transcript.ToolInput
   149→			if err := json.Unmarshal(block.Input, &input); err != nil {
   150→				continue
   151→			}
   152→
   153→			file := input.FilePath
   154→			if file == "" {
   155→				file = input.NotebookPath
   156→			}
   157→
   158→			if file != "" && !fileSet[file] {
   159→				fileSet[file] = true
   160→				files = append(files, file)
   161→			}
   162→		}
   163→	}
   164→
   165→	return files
   166→}
   167→
   168→// CalculateTokenUsage calculates token usage from a Factory AI Droid transcript.
   169→// Due to streaming, multiple transcript rows may share the same message.id.
   170→// We deduplicate by taking the row with the highest output_tokens for each message.id.
   171→func CalculateTokenUsage(transcriptLines []TranscriptLine) *agent.TokenUsage {
   172→	// Map from message.id to the usage with highest output_tokens
   173→	usageByMessageID := make(map[string]messageUsage)
   174→
   175→	for _, line := range transcriptLines {
   176→		if line.Type != "assistant" {
   177→			continue
   178→		}
   179→
   180→		var msg messageWithUsage
   181→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   182→			continue
   183→		}
   184→
   185→		if msg.ID == "" {
   186→			continue
   187→		}
   188→
   189→		// Keep the entry with highest output_tokens (final streaming state)
   190→		existing, exists := usageByMessageID[msg.ID]
   191→		if !exists || msg.Usage.OutputTokens > existing.OutputTokens {
   192→			usageByMessageID[msg.ID] = msg.Usage
   193→		}
   194→	}
   195→
   196→	// Sum up all unique messages
   197→	usage := &agent.TokenUsage{
   198→		APICallCount: len(usageByMessageID),
   199→	}
   200→	for _, u := range usageByMessageID {
   201→		usage.InputTokens += u.InputTokens
   202→		usage.CacheCreationTokens += u.CacheCreationInputTokens
   203→		usage.CacheReadTokens += u.CacheReadInputTokens
   204→		usage.OutputTokens += u.OutputTokens
   205→	}
   206→
   207→	return usage
   208→}
   209→
   210→// CalculateTokenUsageFromFile calculates token usage from a transcript file.
   211→// If startLine > 0, only considers lines from startLine onwards.
   212→func CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(path string, startLine int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   213→	if path == "" {
   214→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}, nil
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	lines, _, err := ParseDroidTranscript(path, startLine)
   218→	if err != nil {
   219→		return nil, err
   220→	}
   221→
   222→	return CalculateTokenUsage(lines), nil
   223→}
   224→
   225→// ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs extracts agent IDs from Task tool results in a transcript.
   226→// When a Task tool completes, the tool_result contains "agentId: <id>" in its content.
   227→// Returns a map of agentID -> toolUseID for all spawned agents.
   228→func ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(transcriptLines []TranscriptLine) map[string]string {
   229→	agentIDs := make(map[string]string)
   230→
   231→	for _, line := range transcriptLines {
   232→		if line.Type != "user" {
   233→			continue
   234→		}
   235→
   236→		// Parse as array of content blocks (tool results)
   237→		var contentBlocks []struct {
   238→			Type      string          `json:"type"`
   239→			ToolUseID string          `json:"tool_use_id"`
   240→			Content   json.RawMessage `json:"content"`
   241→		}
   242→
   243→		var msg struct {
   244→			Content json.RawMessage `json:"content"`
   245→		}
   246→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   247→			continue
   248→		}
   249→
   250→		if err := json.Unmarshal(msg.Content, &contentBlocks); err != nil {
   251→			continue
   252→		}
   253→
   254→		for _, block := range contentBlocks {
   255→			if block.Type != "tool_result" {
   256→				continue
   257→			}
   258→
   259→			// Content can be a string or array of text blocks
   260→			var textContent string
   261→
   262→			// Try as array of text blocks first
   263→			var textBlocks []struct {
   264→				Type string `json:"type"`
   265→				Text string `json:"text"`
   266→			}
   267→			if err := json.Unmarshal(block.Content, &textBlocks); err == nil {
   268→				var sb strings.Builder
   269→				for _, tb := range textBlocks {
   270→					if tb.Type == "text" {
   271→						sb.WriteString(tb.Text + "\n")
   272→					}
   273→				}
   274→				textContent = sb.String()
   275→			} else {
   276→				// Try as plain string
   277→				var str string
   278→				if err := json.Unmarshal(block.Content, &str); err == nil {
   279→					textContent = str
   280→				}
   281→			}
   282→
   283→			// Look for agentId in the text
   284→			if agentID := extractAgentIDFromText(textContent); agentID != "" {
   285→				agentIDs[agentID] = block.ToolUseID
   286→			}
   287→		}
   288→	}
   289→
   290→	return agentIDs
   291→}
   292→
   293→// extractAgentIDFromText extracts an agent ID from text containing "agentId: <id>".
   294→func extractAgentIDFromText(text string) string {
   295→	const prefix = "agentId: "
   296→	idx := strings.Index(text, prefix)
   297→	if idx == -1 {
   298→		return ""
   299→	}
   300→
   301→	// Extract the ID (alphanumeric characters after the prefix)
   302→	start := idx + len(prefix)
   303→	end := start
   304→	for end < len(text) && (text[end] >= 'a' && text[end] <= 'z' ||
   305→		text[end] >= 'A' && text[end] <= 'Z' ||
   306→		text[end] >= '0' && text[end] <= '9') {
   307→		end++
   308→	}
   309→
   310→	if end > start {
   311→		return text[start:end]
   312→	}
   313→	return ""
   314→}
   315→
   316→// CalculateTotalTokenUsageFromTranscript calculates token usage for a turn, including subagents.
   317→// It parses the main transcript from startLine, extracts spawned agent IDs,
   318→// and calculates their token usage from transcripts in subagentsDir.
   319→func CalculateTotalTokenUsageFromTranscript(transcriptPath string, startLine int, subagentsDir string) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   320→	if transcriptPath == "" {
   321→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}, nil
   322→	}
   323→
   324→	// Parse transcript once using Droid-specific parser
   325→	parsed, _, err := ParseDroidTranscript(transcriptPath, startLine)
   326→	if err != nil {
   327→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   328→	}
   329→
   330→	// Calculate token usage from parsed transcript
   331→	mainUsage := CalculateTokenUsage(parsed)
   332→
   333→	// Extract spawned agent IDs from the same parsed transcript
   334→	agentIDs := ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(parsed)
   335→
   336→	// Calculate subagent token usage
   337→	if len(agentIDs) > 0 {
   338→		subagentUsage := &agent.TokenUsage{}
   339→		for agentID := range agentIDs {
   340→			agentPath := filepath.Join(subagentsDir, fmt.Sprintf("agent-%s.jsonl", agentID))
   341→			agentUsage, err := CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(agentPath, 0)
   342→			if err != nil {
   343→				// Agent transcript may not exist yet or may have been cleaned up
   344→				continue
   345→			}
   346→			subagentUsage.InputTokens += agentUsage.InputTokens
   347→			subagentUsage.CacheCreationTokens += agentUsage.CacheCreationTokens
   348→			subagentUsage.CacheReadTokens += agentUsage.CacheReadTokens
   349→			subagentUsage.OutputTokens += agentUsage.OutputTokens
   350→			subagentUsage.APICallCount += agentUsage.APICallCount
   351→		}
   352→		if subagentUsage.APICallCount > 0 {
   353→			mainUsage.SubagentTokens = subagentUsage
   354→		}
   355→	}
   356→
   357→	return mainUsage, nil
   358→}
   359→
   360→// ExtractAllModifiedFilesFromTranscript extracts files modified by both the main agent and
   361→// any subagents spawned via the Task tool. It parses the main transcript from
   362→// startLine, collects modified files from the main agent, then reads each
   363→// subagent's transcript from subagentsDir to collect their modified files too.
   364→// The result is a deduplicated list of all modified file paths.
   365→func ExtractAllModifiedFilesFromTranscript(transcriptPath string, startLine int, subagentsDir string) ([]string, error) {
   366→	if transcriptPath == "" {
   367→		return nil, nil
   368→	}
   369→
   370→	// Parse main transcript once using Droid-specific parser
   371→	parsed, _, err := ParseDroidTranscript(transcriptPath, startLine)
   372→	if err != nil {
   373→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   374→	}
   375→
   376→	// Collect modified files from main agent (already deduplicated)
   377→	files := ExtractModifiedFiles(parsed)
   378→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool, len(files))
   379→	for _, f := range files {
   380→		fileSet[f] = true
   381→	}
   382→
   383→	// Find spawned subagents and collect their modified files
   384→	agentIDs := ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(parsed)
   385→	for agentID := range agentIDs {
   386→		agentPath := filepath.Join(subagentsDir, fmt.Sprintf("agent-%s.jsonl", agentID))
   387→		agentLines, _, agentErr := ParseDroidTranscript(agentPath, 0)
   388→		if agentErr != nil {
   389→			// Subagent transcript may not exist yet or may have been cleaned up
   390→			continue
   391→		}
   392→		for _, f := range ExtractModifiedFiles(agentLines) {
   393→			if !fileSet[f] {
   394→				fileSet[f] = true
   395→				files = append(files, f)
   396→			}
   397→		}
   398→	}
   399→
   400→	return files, nil
   401→}
   402→

    32→// testBinaryPath holds the path to the CLI binary built once in TestMain.
    33→// All tests share this binary to avoid repeated builds.

    35→
    36→// getTestBinary returns the path to the shared test binary.

    41→	}
    42→	return testBinaryPath

    45→// TestEnv manages an isolated test environment for integration tests.
    46→type TestEnv struct {
    47→	T                *testing.T
    48→	RepoDir          string
    49→	ClaudeProjectDir string
    50→	GeminiProjectDir string
    51→	SessionCounter   int
    52→}
    53→
    54→// NewTestEnv creates a new isolated test environment.
    55→// It creates temp directories for the git repo and agent project files.
    56→// Note: Does NOT change working directory to allow parallel test execution.
    57→// Note: Does NOT use t.Setenv to allow parallel test execution - CLI commands
    58→// receive the env var via cmd.Env instead.
    59→func NewTestEnv(t *testing.T) *TestEnv {
    60→	t.Helper()
    61→
    62→	// Resolve symlinks on macOS where /var -> /private/var
    63→	// This ensures the CLI subprocess and test use consistent paths
    64→	repoDir := t.TempDir()
    65→	if resolved, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(repoDir); err == nil {
    66→		repoDir = resolved
    67→	}
    68→	claudeProjectDir := t.TempDir()
    69→	if resolved, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(claudeProjectDir); err == nil {
    70→		claudeProjectDir = resolved
    71→	}
    72→	geminiProjectDir := t.TempDir()
    73→	if resolved, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(geminiProjectDir); err == nil {
    74→		geminiProjectDir = resolved
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	env := &TestEnv{
    78→		T:                t,
    79→		RepoDir:          repoDir,
    80→		ClaudeProjectDir: claudeProjectDir,
    81→		GeminiProjectDir: geminiProjectDir,
    82→	}
    83→
    84→	// Note: Don't use t.Setenv here - it's incompatible with t.Parallel()
    85→	// CLI commands receive ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR or ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR via cmd.Env instead
    86→
    87→	return env
    88→}
    89→
    90→// Cleanup is a no-op retained for backwards compatibility.
    91→//
    92→// Previously this method restored the working directory after NewTestEnv changed it.
    93→// With the refactor to remove os.Chdir from NewTestEnv:
    94→// - Temp directories are now cleaned up automatically by t.TempDir()
    95→// - Working directory is never changed, so no restoration is needed
    96→//
    97→// This method is kept to avoid breaking existing tests that call defer env.Cleanup().
    98→// New tests should not call this method as it serves no purpose.
    99→//
   100→// Deprecated: This method is a no-op and will be removed in a future version.
   101→func (env *TestEnv) Cleanup() {
   102→	// No-op - temp dirs are cleaned up by t.TempDir()
   103→}
