   178→// NewFeatureBranchEnv creates a TestEnv ready for session testing.
   179→// It initializes the repo, creates an initial commit on main,

   181→// for session and rewind tests since Entire tracking skips main/master.
   182→func NewFeatureBranchEnv(t *testing.T, strategyName string) *TestEnv {

   197→// RunForAllStrategies runs a test function for each strategy in parallel.
   198→// This reduces boilerplate for tests that need to verify behavior across all strategies.
   199→// Each subtest gets its own TestEnv with a feature branch ready for testing.
   200→func RunForAllStrategies(t *testing.T, testFn func(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, strategyName string)) {
   201→	t.Helper()
   202→	for _, strat := range AllStrategies() {
   203→		strat := strat // capture for parallel
   204→		t.Run(strat, func(t *testing.T) {
   205→			t.Parallel()
   206→			env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strat)
   207→			testFn(t, env, strat)
   208→		})
   209→	}
   210→}

   212→// RunForAllStrategiesWithRepoEnv runs a test function for each strategy in parallel,
   213→// using NewRepoWithCommit instead of NewFeatureBranchEnv. Use this for tests
   214→// that need to test behavior on the main branch.
   215→func RunForAllStrategiesWithRepoEnv(t *testing.T, testFn func(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, strategyName string)) {
   216→	t.Helper()
   217→	for _, strat := range AllStrategies() {
   218→		strat := strat // capture for parallel
   219→		t.Run(strat, func(t *testing.T) {
   220→			t.Parallel()
   221→			env := NewRepoWithCommit(t, strat)
   222→			testFn(t, env, strat)
   223→		})
   224→	}
   225→}

   231→	t.Helper()

   242→// RunForStrategiesSequential runs a test function for specific strategies sequentially.
   243→// Use this for tests that cannot be parallelized (e.g., tests using os.Chdir).
   244→// The strategies parameter allows testing a subset of strategies.
   245→func RunForStrategiesSequential(t *testing.T, strategies []string, testFn func(t *testing.T, strategyName string)) {
   246→	t.Helper()
   247→	for _, strat := range strategies {
   248→		t.Run(strat, func(t *testing.T) {

   250→		})

   254→// InitRepo initializes a git repository in the test environment.

   257→
   258→	repo, err := git.PlainInit(env.RepoDir, false)
   259→	if err != nil {
   260→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
   261→	}
   262→
   263→	// Configure git user for commits
   264→	cfg, err := repo.Config()
   265→	if err != nil {
   266→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get repo config: %v", err)
   267→	}
   268→	cfg.User.Name = "Test User"
   269→	cfg.User.Email = "test@example.com"
   270→
   271→	// Disable GPG signing for test commits (prevents failures if user has commit.gpgsign=true globally)
   272→	if cfg.Raw == nil {
   273→		cfg.Raw = config.New()
   274→	}
   275→	cfg.Raw.Section("commit").SetOption("gpgsign", "false")
   276→
   277→	if err := repo.SetConfig(cfg); err != nil {
   278→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to set repo config: %v", err)
   279→	}
   280→}

   306→// initEntireInternal is the common implementation for InitEntire variants.
   307→func (env *TestEnv) initEntireInternal(strategyName string, agentName agent.AgentName, strategyOptions map[string]any) {
   308→	env.T.Helper()
   309→
   310→	// Create .entire directory structure
   311→	entireDir := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".entire")
   312→	if err := os.MkdirAll(entireDir, 0o755); err != nil {
   313→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to create .entire directory: %v", err)
   314→	}
   315→
   316→	// Create tmp directory
   317→	tmpDir := filepath.Join(entireDir, "tmp")
   318→	if err := os.MkdirAll(tmpDir, 0o755); err != nil {
   319→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to create .entire/tmp directory: %v", err)
   320→	}
   321→
   322→	// Write settings.json
   323→	settings := map[string]any{
   324→		"strategy":  strategyName,
   325→		"local_dev": true, // Note: git-triggered hooks won't work (path is relative); tests call hooks via getTestBinary() instead
   326→	}
   327→	// Only add agent if specified (otherwise defaults to claude-code)
   328→	if agentName != "" {
   329→		settings["agent"] = string(agentName)
   330→	}
   331→	if strategyOptions != nil {
   332→		settings["strategy_options"] = strategyOptions
   333→	}
   334→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(settings, "", "  ")
   335→	if err != nil {
   336→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to marshal settings: %v", err)
   337→	}
   338→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(entireDir, paths.SettingsFileName)
   339→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, data, 0o644); err != nil {
   340→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write %s: %v", paths.SettingsFileName, err)
   341→	}
   342→}
   343→
   344→// WriteFile creates a file with the given content in the test repo.
   345→// It creates parent directories as needed.
   346→func (env *TestEnv) WriteFile(path, content string) {
   347→	env.T.Helper()
   348→
   349→	fullPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, path)
   350→
   351→	// Create parent directories
   352→	dir := filepath.Dir(fullPath)
   353→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o755); err != nil {
   354→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to create directory %s: %v", dir, err)
   355→	}
   356→
   357→	if err := os.WriteFile(fullPath, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {
   358→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write file %s: %v", path, err)
   359→	}

   501→// GitCommitWithMultipleSessions creates a commit with multiple Entire-Session trailers.
   502→// This simulates merge commits that combine work from multiple sessions.
   503→func (env *TestEnv) GitCommitWithMultipleSessions(message string, sessionIDs []string) {
   504→	env.T.Helper()
   505→
   506→	// Format message with multiple session trailers
   507→	fullMessage := message + "\n\n"
   508→	var fullMessageSb404 strings.Builder
   509→	for _, sessionID := range sessionIDs {
   510→		fullMessageSb404.WriteString("Entire-Session: " + sessionID + "\n")
   511→	}
   512→	fullMessage += fullMessageSb404.String()
   513→
   514→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   515→	if err != nil {
   516→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   517→	}
   518→
   519→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   520→	if err != nil {
   521→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   522→	}
   523→
   524→	_, err = worktree.Commit(fullMessage, &git.CommitOptions{
   525→		Author: &object.Signature{
   526→			Name:  "Test User",
   527→			Email: "test@example.com",
   528→			When:  time.Now(),
   529→		},
   530→	})
   531→	if err != nil {
   532→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
   533→	}
   534→}

   578→// GetGitLog returns a list of commit hashes from HEAD.
   579→func (env *TestEnv) GetGitLog() []string {
   580→	env.T.Helper()
   581→
   582→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   583→	if err != nil {
   584→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   585→	}
   586→
   587→	head, err := repo.Head()
   588→	if err != nil {
   589→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get HEAD: %v", err)
   590→	}
   591→
   592→	commitIter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: head.Hash()})
   593→	if err != nil {
   594→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get log: %v", err)
   595→	}
   596→
   597→	var commits []string
   598→	err = commitIter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
   599→		commits = append(commits, c.Hash.String())
   600→		return nil
   601→	})
   602→	if err != nil {
   603→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to iterate commits: %v", err)
   604→	}
   605→
   606→	return commits
   607→}

   609→// GitCheckoutNewBranch creates and checks out a new branch.
   610→// Uses git CLI instead of go-git to work around go-git v5 bug where Checkout
   611→// deletes untracked files (see https://github.com/go-git/go-git/issues/970).
   612→func (env *TestEnv) GitCheckoutNewBranch(branchName string) {
   613→	env.T.Helper()
   614→
   615→	cmd := exec.Command("git", "checkout", "-b", branchName)
   616→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   617→	if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   618→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to checkout new branch %s: %v\nOutput: %s", branchName, err, output)
   619→	}
   620→}
   621→
   622→// GetCurrentBranch returns the current branch name.
   623→func (env *TestEnv) GetCurrentBranch() string {
   624→	env.T.Helper()
   625→
   626→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   627→	if err != nil {
   628→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   629→	}
   630→
   631→	head, err := repo.Head()
   632→	if err != nil {
   633→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get HEAD: %v", err)
   634→	}
   635→
   636→	if !head.Name().IsBranch() {
   637→		return "" // Detached HEAD
   638→	}
   639→
   640→	return head.Name().Short()
   641→}

   643→// RewindPoint mirrors strategy.RewindPoint for test assertions.
   644→type RewindPoint struct {
   645→	ID               string
   646→	Message          string
   647→	MetadataDir      string
   648→	Date             time.Time
   649→	IsTaskCheckpoint bool
   650→	ToolUseID        string
   651→	IsLogsOnly       bool
   652→	CondensationID   string
   653→}
   654→
   655→// GetRewindPoints returns available rewind points using the CLI.
   656→func (env *TestEnv) GetRewindPoints() []RewindPoint {
   657→	env.T.Helper()
   658→
   659→	// Run rewind --list using the shared binary
   660→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "rewind", "--list")
   661→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   662→	cmd.Env = env.cliEnv()
   663→
   664→	output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
   665→	if err != nil {
   666→		env.T.Fatalf("rewind --list failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
   667→	}
   668→
   669→	// Parse JSON output
   670→	var jsonPoints []struct {
   671→		ID               string `json:"id"`
   672→		Message          string `json:"message"`
   673→		MetadataDir      string `json:"metadata_dir"`
   674→		Date             string `json:"date"`
   675→		IsTaskCheckpoint bool   `json:"is_task_checkpoint"`
   676→		ToolUseID        string `json:"tool_use_id"`
   677→		IsLogsOnly       bool   `json:"is_logs_only"`
   678→		CondensationID   string `json:"condensation_id"`
   679→	}
   680→
   681→	if err := json.Unmarshal(output, &jsonPoints); err != nil {
   682→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to parse rewind points: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
   683→	}
   684→
   685→	points := make([]RewindPoint, len(jsonPoints))
   686→	for i, jp := range jsonPoints {
   687→		date, _ := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, jp.Date)
   688→		points[i] = RewindPoint{
   689→			ID:               jp.ID,
   690→			Message:          jp.Message,
   691→			MetadataDir:      jp.MetadataDir,
   692→			Date:             date,
   693→			IsTaskCheckpoint: jp.IsTaskCheckpoint,
   694→			ToolUseID:        jp.ToolUseID,
   695→			IsLogsOnly:       jp.IsLogsOnly,
   696→			CondensationID:   jp.CondensationID,
   697→		}
   698→	}
   699→
   700→	return points
   701→}
   702→
   703→// Rewind performs a rewind to the specified commit ID using the CLI.
   704→func (env *TestEnv) Rewind(commitID string) error {
   705→	env.T.Helper()
   706→
   707→	// Run rewind --to <commitID> using the shared binary
   708→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "rewind", "--to", commitID)
   709→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   710→	cmd.Env = env.cliEnv()
   711→
   712→	output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
   713→	if err != nil {
   714→		return errors.New("rewind failed: " + string(output))
   715→	}
   716→
   717→	env.T.Logf("Rewind output: %s", output)
   718→	return nil
   719→}

   759→// BranchExists checks if a branch exists in the repository.
   760→func (env *TestEnv) BranchExists(branchName string) bool {
   761→	env.T.Helper()
   762→
   763→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   764→	if err != nil {
   765→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   766→	}
   767→
   768→	refs, err := repo.References()
   769→	if err != nil {
   770→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get references: %v", err)
   771→	}
   772→
   773→	found := false
   774→	_ = refs.ForEach(func(ref *plumbing.Reference) error {
   775→		if ref.Name().Short() == branchName {
   776→			found = true
   777→		}
   778→		return nil
   779→	})
   780→
   781→	return found
   782→}

   784→// GetCommitMessage returns the commit message for the given commit hash.

   801→
   802→// FileExistsInBranch checks if a file exists in a specific branch's tree.
   803→func (env *TestEnv) FileExistsInBranch(branchName, filePath string) bool {
   804→	env.T.Helper()
   805→
   806→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   807→	if err != nil {
   808→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   809→	}
   810→
   811→	// Get the branch reference
   812→	ref, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchName), true)
   813→	if err != nil {
   814→		// Try as a remote-style ref
   815→		ref, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.ReferenceName("refs/heads/"+branchName), true)
   816→		if err != nil {
   817→			return false
   818→		}
   819→	}
   820→
   821→	// Get the commit
   822→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   823→	if err != nil {
   824→		return false
   825→	}
   826→
   827→	// Get the tree
   828→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   829→	if err != nil {
   830→		return false
   831→	}
   832→
   833→	// Check if file exists
   834→	_, err = tree.File(filePath)
   835→	return err == nil
   836→}
   837→
   838→// ReadFileFromBranch reads a file's content from a specific branch's tree.
   839→// Returns the content and true if found, empty string and false if not found.
   840→func (env *TestEnv) ReadFileFromBranch(branchName, filePath string) (string, bool) {
   841→	env.T.Helper()
   842→
   843→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   844→	if err != nil {
   845→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   846→	}
   847→
   848→	// Get the branch reference
   849→	ref, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchName), true)
   850→	if err != nil {
   851→		// Try as a remote-style ref
   852→		ref, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.ReferenceName("refs/heads/"+branchName), true)
   853→		if err != nil {
   854→			return "", false
   855→		}
   856→	}
   857→
   858→	// Get the commit
   859→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   860→	if err != nil {
   861→		return "", false
   862→	}
   863→
   864→	// Get the tree
   865→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   866→	if err != nil {
   867→		return "", false
   868→	}
   869→
   870→	// Get the file
   871→	file, err := tree.File(filePath)
   872→	if err != nil {
   873→		return "", false
   874→	}
   875→
   876→	// Get the content
   877→	content, err := file.Contents()
   878→	if err != nil {
   879→		return "", false
   880→	}
   881→
   882→	return content, true
   883→}

   885→// GetLatestCommitMessageOnBranch returns the commit message of the latest commit on the given branch.

   902→	if err != nil {
   903→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get commit object: %v", err)
   904→	}
   905→
   906→	return commit.Message
   907→}
   908→
   909→// GitCommitWithShadowHooks stages and commits files, simulating the prepare-commit-msg
   910→// and post-commit hooks as a human (with TTY). This is the default for tests.
   911→func (env *TestEnv) GitCommitWithShadowHooks(message string, files ...string) {
   912→	env.T.Helper()
   913→	env.gitCommitWithShadowHooks(message, true, files...)
   914→}
   915→
   916→// GitCommitWithShadowHooksAsAgent is like GitCommitWithShadowHooks but simulates
   917→// an agent commit (no TTY). This triggers the fast path in PrepareCommitMsg that
   918→// skips content detection and interactive prompts for ACTIVE sessions.
   919→func (env *TestEnv) GitCommitWithShadowHooksAsAgent(message string, files ...string) {
   920→	env.T.Helper()
   921→	env.gitCommitWithShadowHooks(message, false, files...)
   922→}
   923→
   924→// gitCommitWithShadowHooks is the shared implementation for committing with shadow hooks.
   925→// When simulateTTY is true, sets ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1 to simulate a human at the terminal.
   926→// When false, filters it out to simulate an agent subprocess (no controlling terminal).
   927→func (env *TestEnv) gitCommitWithShadowHooks(message string, simulateTTY bool, files ...string) {
   928→	env.T.Helper()
   929→
   930→	// Stage files using go-git
   931→	for _, file := range files {
   932→		env.GitAdd(file)
   933→	}
   934→
   935→	// Create a temp file for the commit message (prepare-commit-msg hook modifies this)
   936→	msgFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "COMMIT_EDITMSG")
   937→	if err := os.WriteFile(msgFile, []byte(message), 0o644); err != nil {
   938→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write commit message file: %v", err)
   939→	}
   940→
   941→	// Run prepare-commit-msg hook using the shared binary.
   942→	// Pass source="message" to match real `git commit -m` behavior.
   943→	prepCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "prepare-commit-msg", msgFile, "message")
   944→	prepCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   945→	if simulateTTY {
   946→		// Simulate human at terminal: ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1 makes hasTTY() return true
   947→		// and askConfirmTTY() return defaultYes without reading from /dev/tty.
   948→		prepCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(), "ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1")
   949→	} else {
   950→		// Simulate agent: ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=0 makes hasTTY() return false,
   951→		// triggering the fast path that adds trailers for ACTIVE sessions.
   952→		prepCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(), "ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=0")
   953→	}
   954→	if output, err := prepCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   955→		env.T.Logf("prepare-commit-msg output: %s", output)
   956→		// Don't fail - hook may silently succeed
   957→	}
   958→
   959→	// Read the modified message
   960→	modifiedMsg, err := os.ReadFile(msgFile)
   961→	if err != nil {
   962→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to read modified commit message: %v", err)
   963→	}
   964→
   965→	// Create the commit using go-git with the modified message
   966→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   967→	if err != nil {
   968→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   969→	}
   970→
   971→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   972→	if err != nil {
   973→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   974→	}
   975→
   976→	_, err = worktree.Commit(string(modifiedMsg), &git.CommitOptions{
   977→		Author: &object.Signature{
   978→			Name:  "Test User",
   979→			Email: "test@example.com",
   980→			When:  time.Now(),
   981→		},
   982→	})
   983→	if err != nil {
   984→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
   985→	}
   986→
   987→	// Run post-commit hook using the shared binary
   988→	// This triggers condensation if the commit has an Entire-Checkpoint trailer
   989→	postCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "post-commit")
   990→	postCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   991→	if output, err := postCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   992→		env.T.Logf("post-commit output: %s", output)
   993→		// Don't fail - hook may silently succeed
   994→	}
   995→}
   996→
   997→// GitCommitAmendWithShadowHooks amends the last commit with shadow hooks.
   998→// This simulates `git commit --amend` with the prepare-commit-msg and post-commit hooks.
   999→// The prepare-commit-msg hook is called with "commit" source to indicate an amend.
  1000→func (env *TestEnv) GitCommitAmendWithShadowHooks(message string, files ...string) {
  1001→	env.T.Helper()
  1002→
  1003→	// Stage any additional files
  1004→	for _, file := range files {
  1005→		env.GitAdd(file)
  1006→	}
  1007→
  1008→	// Write commit message to temp file
  1009→	msgFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "COMMIT_EDITMSG")
  1010→	if err := os.WriteFile(msgFile, []byte(message), 0o644); err != nil {
  1011→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write commit message file: %v", err)
  1012→	}
  1013→
  1014→	// Run prepare-commit-msg hook with "commit" source (indicates amend).
  1015→	// Set ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1 to simulate human (amend is always a human operation).
  1016→	prepCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "prepare-commit-msg", msgFile, "commit")
  1017→	prepCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
  1018→	prepCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(), "ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1")
  1019→	if output, err := prepCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
  1020→		env.T.Logf("prepare-commit-msg (amend) output: %s", output)
  1021→	}
  1022→
  1023→	// Read the modified message
  1024→	modifiedMsg, err := os.ReadFile(msgFile)
  1025→	if err != nil {
  1026→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to read modified commit message: %v", err)
  1027→	}
  1028→
  1029→	// Amend the commit using go-git
  1030→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
  1031→	if err != nil {
  1032→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
  1033→	}
  1034→
  1035→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
  1036→	if err != nil {
  1037→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
  1038→	}
  1039→
  1040→	_, err = worktree.Commit(string(modifiedMsg), &git.CommitOptions{
  1041→		Author: &object.Signature{
  1042→			Name:  "Test User",
  1043→			Email: "test@example.com",
  1044→			When:  time.Now(),
  1045→		},
  1046→		Amend: true,
  1047→	})
  1048→	if err != nil {
  1049→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to amend commit: %v", err)
  1050→	}
  1051→
  1052→	// Run post-commit hook
  1053→	postCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "post-commit")
  1054→	postCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
  1055→	if output, err := postCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
  1056→		env.T.Logf("post-commit (amend) output: %s", output)
  1057→	}
  1058→}
  1059→
  1060→// GitCommitWithTrailerRemoved stages and commits files, simulating what happens when
  1061→// a user removes the Entire-Checkpoint trailer during commit message editing.
  1062→// This tests the opt-out behavior where removing the trailer skips condensation.
  1063→func (env *TestEnv) GitCommitWithTrailerRemoved(message string, files ...string) {
  1064→	env.T.Helper()
  1065→
  1066→	// Stage files using go-git
  1067→	for _, file := range files {
  1068→		env.GitAdd(file)
  1069→	}
  1070→
  1071→	// Create a temp file for the commit message (prepare-commit-msg hook modifies this)
  1072→	msgFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "COMMIT_EDITMSG")
  1073→	if err := os.WriteFile(msgFile, []byte(message), 0o644); err != nil {
  1074→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write commit message file: %v", err)
  1075→	}
  1076→
  1077→	// Run prepare-commit-msg hook using the shared binary.
  1078→	// Set ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1 to simulate human (this tests the editor flow where
  1079→	// the user removes the trailer before committing).
  1080→	prepCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "prepare-commit-msg", msgFile)
  1081→	prepCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
  1082→	prepCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(), "ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1")
  1083→	if output, err := prepCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
  1084→		env.T.Logf("prepare-commit-msg output: %s", output)
  1085→	}
  1086→
  1087→	// Read the modified message (with trailer added by hook)
  1088→	modifiedMsg, err := os.ReadFile(msgFile)
  1089→	if err != nil {
  1090→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to read modified commit message: %v", err)
  1091→	}
  1092→
  1093→	// REMOVE the Entire-Checkpoint trailer (simulating user editing the message)
  1094→	lines := strings.Split(string(modifiedMsg), "\n")
  1095→	var cleanedLines []string
  1096→	for _, line := range lines {
  1097→		// Skip the trailer and the comments about it
  1098→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:") {
  1099→			continue
  1100→		}
  1101→		if strings.Contains(line, "Remove the Entire-Checkpoint trailer") {
  1102→			continue
  1103→		}
  1104→		if strings.Contains(line, "trailer will be added to your next commit") {
  1105→			continue
  1106→		}
  1107→		cleanedLines = append(cleanedLines, line)
  1108→	}
  1109→	cleanedMsg := strings.TrimRight(strings.Join(cleanedLines, "\n"), "\n") + "\n"
  1110→
  1111→	// Create the commit using go-git with the cleaned message (no trailer)
  1112→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
  1113→	if err != nil {
  1114→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
  1115→	}
  1116→
  1117→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
  1118→	if err != nil {
  1119→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
  1120→	}
  1121→
  1122→	_, err = worktree.Commit(cleanedMsg, &git.CommitOptions{
  1123→		Author: &object.Signature{
  1124→			Name:  "Test User",
  1125→			Email: "test@example.com",
  1126→			When:  time.Now(),
  1127→		},
  1128→	})
  1129→	if err != nil {
  1130→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
  1131→	}
  1132→
  1133→	// Run post-commit hook - since trailer was removed, no condensation should happen
  1134→	postCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "post-commit")
  1135→	postCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
  1136→	if output, err := postCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
  1137→		env.T.Logf("post-commit output: %s", output)
  1138→	}
  1139→}

  1178→	}

  1181→	}

  1199→
  1200→	_, err = worktree.Commit(string(modifiedMsg), &git.CommitOptions{

  1210→
  1211→	// Run post-commit hook
  1212→	postCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "post-commit")
  1213→	postCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
  1214→	if output, err := postCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {

  1219→// ListBranchesWithPrefix returns all branches that start with the given prefix.
  1220→func (env *TestEnv) ListBranchesWithPrefix(prefix string) []string {
  1221→	env.T.Helper()
  1222→
  1223→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
  1224→	if err != nil {
  1225→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
  1226→	}
  1227→
  1228→	refs, err := repo.References()
  1229→	if err != nil {
  1230→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get references: %v", err)
  1231→	}
  1232→
  1233→	var branches []string
  1234→	_ = refs.ForEach(func(ref *plumbing.Reference) error {
  1235→		name := ref.Name().Short()
  1236→		if len(name) >= len(prefix) && name[:len(prefix)] == prefix {
  1237→			branches = append(branches, name)
  1238→		}
  1239→		return nil
  1240→	})
  1241→
  1242→	return branches
  1243→}
  1244→
  1245→// GetLatestCheckpointID returns the most recent checkpoint ID from the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
  1246→// This is used by tests that previously extracted the checkpoint ID from commit message trailers.
  1247→// Now that active branch commits are clean (no trailers), we get the ID from the sessions branch.
  1248→// Fatals if the checkpoint ID cannot be found, with detailed context about what was found.
  1249→func (env *TestEnv) GetLatestCheckpointID() string {
  1250→	env.T.Helper()
  1251→
  1252→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
  1253→	if err != nil {
  1254→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
  1255→	}
  1256→
  1257→	// Get the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
  1258→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1259→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1260→	if err != nil {
  1261→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get %s branch: %v", paths.MetadataBranchName, err)
  1262→	}
  1263→
  1264→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
  1265→	if err != nil {
  1266→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get commit: %v", err)
  1267→	}
  1268→
  1269→	// Extract checkpoint ID from commit message
  1270→	// Format: "Checkpoint: <12-hex-char-id>\n\nSession: ...\nStrategy: ..."
  1271→	for _, line := range strings.Split(commit.Message, "\n") {
  1272→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
  1273→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "Checkpoint: ") {
  1274→			return strings.TrimPrefix(line, "Checkpoint: ")
  1275→		}
  1276→	}
  1277→
  1278→	env.T.Fatalf("could not find checkpoint ID in %s branch commit message:\n%s",
  1279→		paths.MetadataBranchName, commit.Message)
  1280→	return ""
  1281→}
  1282→
  1283→// TryGetLatestCheckpointID returns the most recent checkpoint ID from the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
  1284→// Returns empty string if the branch doesn't exist or has no checkpoint commits yet.
  1285→// Use this when you need to check if a checkpoint exists without failing the test.
  1286→func (env *TestEnv) TryGetLatestCheckpointID() string {
  1287→	env.T.Helper()
  1288→
  1289→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
  1290→	if err != nil {
  1291→		return ""
  1292→	}
  1293→
  1294→	// Get the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
  1295→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1296→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1297→	if err != nil {
  1298→		return ""
  1299→	}
  1300→
  1301→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
  1302→	if err != nil {
  1303→		return ""
  1304→	}
  1305→
  1306→	// Extract checkpoint ID from commit message
  1307→	// Format: "Checkpoint: <12-hex-char-id>\n\nSession: ...\nStrategy: ..."
  1308→	for _, line := range strings.Split(commit.Message, "\n") {
  1309→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
  1310→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "Checkpoint: ") {
  1311→			return strings.TrimPrefix(line, "Checkpoint: ")
  1312→		}
  1313→	}
  1314→
  1315→	return ""
  1316→}
  1317→
  1318→// GetLatestCondensationID is an alias for GetLatestCheckpointID for backwards compatibility.
  1319→func (env *TestEnv) GetLatestCondensationID() string {
  1320→	return env.GetLatestCheckpointID()
  1321→}
  1322→
  1323→// GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage extracts the Entire-Checkpoint trailer from a commit message.
  1324→// Returns empty string if no trailer found.
  1325→func (env *TestEnv) GetCheckpointIDFromCommitMessage(commitSHA string) string {
  1326→	env.T.Helper()
  1327→
  1328→	msg := env.GetCommitMessage(commitSHA)
  1329→	cpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(msg)
  1330→	if !found {
  1331→		return ""
  1332→	}
  1333→	return cpID.String()
  1334→}
  1335→
  1336→// GetLatestCheckpointIDFromHistory walks backwards from HEAD on the active branch
  1337→// and returns the checkpoint ID from the first commit that has an Entire-Checkpoint trailer.
  1338→// This verifies that condensation actually happened (commit has trailer) without relying
  1339→// on timestamp-based matching.
  1340→func (env *TestEnv) GetLatestCheckpointIDFromHistory() string {
  1341→	env.T.Helper()
  1342→
  1343→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
  1344→	if err != nil {
  1345→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
  1346→	}
  1347→
  1348→	head, err := repo.Head()
  1349→	if err != nil {
  1350→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get HEAD: %v", err)
  1351→	}
  1352→
  1353→	commitIter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: head.Hash()})
  1354→	if err != nil {
  1355→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to iterate commits: %v", err)
  1356→	}
  1357→
  1358→	var checkpointID string
  1359→	//nolint:errcheck // ForEach callback handles errors
  1360→	commitIter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
  1361→		if cpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(c.Message); found {
  1362→			checkpointID = cpID.String()
  1363→			return errors.New("stop iteration") // Found it, stop
  1364→		}
  1365→		return nil
  1366→	})
  1367→
  1368→	if checkpointID == "" {
  1369→		env.T.Fatalf("no commit with Entire-Checkpoint trailer found in history")
  1370→	}
  1371→
  1372→	return checkpointID
  1373→}
  1374→
  1375→// ShardedCheckpointPath returns the sharded path for a checkpoint ID.
  1376→// Format: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>
  1377→// Delegates to id.CheckpointID.Path() for consistency.
  1378→func ShardedCheckpointPath(checkpointID string) string {
  1379→	return id.CheckpointID(checkpointID).Path()
  1380→}
  1381→
  1382→// SessionFilePath returns the path to a session file within a checkpoint.
  1383→// Session files are stored in numbered subdirectories using 0-based indexing (e.g., 0/full.jsonl).
  1384→// This function constructs the path for the first (default) session.
  1385→func SessionFilePath(checkpointID string, fileName string) string {
  1386→	return id.CheckpointID(checkpointID).Path() + "/0/" + fileName
  1387→}
  1388→
  1389→// CheckpointSummaryPath returns the path to the root metadata.json (CheckpointSummary) for a checkpoint.
  1390→func CheckpointSummaryPath(checkpointID string) string {
  1391→	return id.CheckpointID(checkpointID).Path() + "/" + paths.MetadataFileName
  1392→}
  1393→
  1394→// SessionMetadataPath returns the path to the session-level metadata.json for a checkpoint.
  1395→func SessionMetadataPath(checkpointID string) string {
  1396→	return SessionFilePath(checkpointID, paths.MetadataFileName)
  1397→}

  1399→// CheckpointValidation contains expected values for checkpoint validation.
  1400→type CheckpointValidation struct {
  1401→	// CheckpointID is the expected checkpoint ID
  1402→	CheckpointID string
  1403→
  1404→	// SessionID is the expected session ID
  1405→	SessionID string
  1406→
  1407→	// Strategy is the expected strategy name
  1408→	Strategy string
  1409→
  1410→	// FilesTouched are the expected files in files_touched
  1411→	FilesTouched []string
  1412→
  1413→	// ExpectedPrompts are strings that should appear in prompt.txt
  1414→	ExpectedPrompts []string
  1415→
  1416→	// ExpectedTranscriptContent are strings that should appear in full.jsonl
  1417→	ExpectedTranscriptContent []string
  1418→
  1419→	// CheckpointsCount is the expected checkpoint count (0 means don't validate)
  1420→	CheckpointsCount int
  1421→}
  1422→
  1423→// ValidateCheckpoint performs comprehensive validation of a checkpoint on the metadata branch.
  1424→// It validates:
  1425→// - Root metadata.json (CheckpointSummary) structure and expected fields
  1426→// - Session metadata.json (CommittedMetadata) structure and expected fields
  1427→// - Transcript file (full.jsonl) is valid JSONL and contains expected content
  1428→// - Content hash file (content_hash.txt) matches SHA256 of transcript
  1429→// - Prompt file (prompt.txt) contains expected prompts
  1430→func (env *TestEnv) ValidateCheckpoint(v CheckpointValidation) {
  1431→	env.T.Helper()
  1432→
  1433→	// Validate root metadata.json (CheckpointSummary)
  1434→	env.validateCheckpointSummary(v)
  1435→
  1436→	// Validate session metadata.json (CommittedMetadata)
  1437→	env.validateSessionMetadata(v)
  1438→
  1439→	// Validate transcript is valid JSONL
  1440→	env.validateTranscriptJSONL(v.CheckpointID, v.ExpectedTranscriptContent)
  1441→
  1442→	// Validate content hash matches transcript
  1443→	env.validateContentHash(v.CheckpointID)
  1444→
  1445→	// Validate prompt.txt contains expected prompts
  1446→	if len(v.ExpectedPrompts) > 0 {
  1447→		env.validatePromptContent(v.CheckpointID, v.ExpectedPrompts)
  1448→	}
  1449→}
  1450→
  1451→// validateCheckpointSummary validates the root metadata.json (CheckpointSummary).
  1452→func (env *TestEnv) validateCheckpointSummary(v CheckpointValidation) {
  1453→	env.T.Helper()
  1454→
  1455→	summaryPath := CheckpointSummaryPath(v.CheckpointID)
  1456→	content, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, summaryPath)
  1457→	if !found {
  1458→		env.T.Fatalf("CheckpointSummary not found at %s", summaryPath)
  1459→	}
  1460→
  1461→	var summary checkpoint.CheckpointSummary
  1462→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &summary); err != nil {
  1463→		env.T.Fatalf("Failed to parse CheckpointSummary: %v\nContent: %s", err, content)
  1464→	}
  1465→
  1466→	// Validate checkpoint_id
  1467→	if summary.CheckpointID.String() != v.CheckpointID {
  1468→		env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary.CheckpointID = %q, want %q", summary.CheckpointID, v.CheckpointID)
  1469→	}
  1470→
  1471→	// Validate strategy
  1472→	if v.Strategy != "" && summary.Strategy != v.Strategy {
  1473→		env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary.Strategy = %q, want %q", summary.Strategy, v.Strategy)
  1474→	}
  1475→
  1476→	// Validate sessions array is populated
  1477→	if len(summary.Sessions) == 0 {
  1478→		env.T.Error("CheckpointSummary.Sessions should have at least one entry")
  1479→	}
  1480→
  1481→	// Validate files_touched
  1482→	if len(v.FilesTouched) > 0 {
  1483→		touchedSet := make(map[string]bool)
  1484→		for _, f := range summary.FilesTouched {
  1485→			touchedSet[f] = true
  1486→		}
  1487→		for _, expected := range v.FilesTouched {
  1488→			if !touchedSet[expected] {
  1489→				env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary.FilesTouched missing %q, got %v", expected, summary.FilesTouched)
  1490→			}
  1491→		}
  1492→	}
  1493→
  1494→	// Validate checkpoints_count
  1495→	if v.CheckpointsCount > 0 && summary.CheckpointsCount != v.CheckpointsCount {
  1496→		env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary.CheckpointsCount = %d, want %d", summary.CheckpointsCount, v.CheckpointsCount)
  1497→	}
  1498→}
  1499→
  1500→// validateSessionMetadata validates the session-level metadata.json (CommittedMetadata).
  1501→func (env *TestEnv) validateSessionMetadata(v CheckpointValidation) {
  1502→	env.T.Helper()
  1503→
  1504→	metadataPath := SessionMetadataPath(v.CheckpointID)
  1505→	content, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, metadataPath)
  1506→	if !found {
  1507→		env.T.Fatalf("Session metadata not found at %s", metadataPath)
  1508→	}
  1509→
  1510→	var metadata checkpoint.CommittedMetadata
  1511→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &metadata); err != nil {
  1512→		env.T.Fatalf("Failed to parse CommittedMetadata: %v\nContent: %s", err, content)
  1513→	}
  1514→
  1515→	// Validate checkpoint_id
  1516→	if metadata.CheckpointID.String() != v.CheckpointID {
  1517→		env.T.Errorf("CommittedMetadata.CheckpointID = %q, want %q", metadata.CheckpointID, v.CheckpointID)
  1518→	}
  1519→
  1520→	// Validate session_id
  1521→	if v.SessionID != "" && metadata.SessionID != v.SessionID {
  1522→		env.T.Errorf("CommittedMetadata.SessionID = %q, want %q", metadata.SessionID, v.SessionID)
  1523→	}
  1524→
  1525→	// Validate strategy
  1526→	if v.Strategy != "" && metadata.Strategy != v.Strategy {
  1527→		env.T.Errorf("CommittedMetadata.Strategy = %q, want %q", metadata.Strategy, v.Strategy)
  1528→	}
  1529→
  1530→	// Validate created_at is not zero
  1531→	if metadata.CreatedAt.IsZero() {
  1532→		env.T.Error("CommittedMetadata.CreatedAt should not be zero")
  1533→	}
  1534→
  1535→	// Validate files_touched
  1536→	if len(v.FilesTouched) > 0 {
  1537→		touchedSet := make(map[string]bool)
  1538→		for _, f := range metadata.FilesTouched {
  1539→			touchedSet[f] = true
  1540→		}
  1541→		for _, expected := range v.FilesTouched {
  1542→			if !touchedSet[expected] {
  1543→				env.T.Errorf("CommittedMetadata.FilesTouched missing %q, got %v", expected, metadata.FilesTouched)
  1544→			}
  1545→		}
  1546→	}
  1547→
  1548→	// Validate checkpoints_count
  1549→	if v.CheckpointsCount > 0 && metadata.CheckpointsCount != v.CheckpointsCount {
  1550→		env.T.Errorf("CommittedMetadata.CheckpointsCount = %d, want %d", metadata.CheckpointsCount, v.CheckpointsCount)
  1551→	}
  1552→}
  1553→
  1554→// validateTranscriptJSONL validates that full.jsonl exists and is valid JSONL.
  1555→func (env *TestEnv) validateTranscriptJSONL(checkpointID string, expectedContent []string) {
  1556→	env.T.Helper()
  1557→
  1558→	transcriptPath := SessionFilePath(checkpointID, paths.TranscriptFileName)
  1559→	content, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, transcriptPath)
  1560→	if !found {
  1561→		env.T.Fatalf("Transcript not found at %s", transcriptPath)
  1562→	}
  1563→
  1564→	// Validate it's valid JSONL (each non-empty line should be valid JSON)
  1565→	lines := strings.Split(content, "\n")
  1566→	validLines := 0
  1567→	for i, line := range lines {
  1568→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
  1569→		if line == "" {
  1570→			continue
  1571→		}
  1572→		validLines++
  1573→		var obj map[string]any
  1574→		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &obj); err != nil {
  1575→			env.T.Errorf("Transcript line %d is not valid JSON: %v\nLine: %s", i+1, err, line)
  1576→		}
  1577→	}
  1578→
  1579→	if validLines == 0 {
  1580→		env.T.Error("Transcript is empty (no valid JSONL lines)")
  1581→	}
  1582→
  1583→	// Validate expected content appears in transcript
  1584→	for _, expected := range expectedContent {
  1585→		if !strings.Contains(content, expected) {
  1586→			env.T.Errorf("Transcript should contain %q", expected)
  1587→		}
  1588→	}
  1589→}
  1590→
  1591→// validateContentHash validates that content_hash.txt matches the SHA256 of the transcript.
  1592→func (env *TestEnv) validateContentHash(checkpointID string) {
  1593→	env.T.Helper()
  1594→
  1595→	// Read transcript
  1596→	transcriptPath := SessionFilePath(checkpointID, paths.TranscriptFileName)
  1597→	transcript, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, transcriptPath)
  1598→	if !found {
  1599→		env.T.Fatalf("Transcript not found at %s", transcriptPath)
  1600→	}
  1601→
  1602→	// Read content hash
  1603→	hashPath := SessionFilePath(checkpointID, "content_hash.txt")
  1604→	storedHash, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, hashPath)
  1605→	if !found {
  1606→		env.T.Fatalf("Content hash not found at %s", hashPath)
  1607→	}
  1608→	storedHash = strings.TrimSpace(storedHash)
  1609→
  1610→	// Calculate expected hash with sha256: prefix (matches format in committed.go)
  1611→	hash := sha256.Sum256([]byte(transcript))
  1612→	expectedHash := "sha256:" + hex.EncodeToString(hash[:])
  1613→
  1614→	if storedHash != expectedHash {
  1615→		env.T.Errorf("Content hash mismatch:\n  stored:   %s\n  expected: %s", storedHash, expectedHash)
  1616→	}
  1617→}
  1618→
  1619→// validatePromptContent validates that prompt.txt contains the expected prompts.
  1620→func (env *TestEnv) validatePromptContent(checkpointID string, expectedPrompts []string) {
  1621→	env.T.Helper()
  1622→
  1623→	promptPath := SessionFilePath(checkpointID, paths.PromptFileName)
  1624→	content, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, promptPath)
  1625→	if !found {
  1626→		env.T.Fatalf("Prompt file not found at %s", promptPath)
  1627→	}
  1628→
  1629→	for _, expected := range expectedPrompts {
  1630→		if !strings.Contains(content, expected) {
  1631→			env.T.Errorf("Prompt file should contain %q\nContent: %s", expected, content)
  1632→		}
  1633→	}
  1634→}
  1635→
  1636→func findModuleRoot() string {
  1637→	// Start from this source file's location and walk up to find go.mod
  1638→	_, thisFile, _, ok := runtime.Caller(0)
  1639→	if !ok {
  1640→		panic("failed to get current file path via runtime.Caller")
  1641→	}
  1642→	dir := filepath.Dir(thisFile)
  1643→
  1644→	for {
  1645→		if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(dir, "go.mod")); err == nil {
  1646→			return dir
  1647→		}
  1648→		parent := filepath.Dir(dir)
  1649→		if parent == dir {
  1650→			panic("could not find go.mod starting from " + thisFile)
  1651→		}
  1652→		dir = parent
  1653→	}
  1654→}
  1655→

    14→// TranscriptBuilder builds realistic JSONL transcripts for testing.
    15→type TranscriptBuilder struct {
    16→	messages       []map[string]interface{}
    17→	toolUseCounter int
    18→}
    19→
    20→// NewTranscriptBuilder creates a new transcript builder.
    21→func NewTranscriptBuilder() *TranscriptBuilder {
    22→	return &TranscriptBuilder{
    23→		messages: make([]map[string]interface{}, 0),
    24→	}
    25→}
    26→
    27→// AddUserMessage adds a user message with string content.
    28→func (b *TranscriptBuilder) AddUserMessage(content string) *TranscriptBuilder {
    29→	b.messages = append(b.messages, map[string]interface{}{
    30→		"uuid":      fmt.Sprintf("user-%d", len(b.messages)+1),
    31→		"type":      "user",
    32→		"message":   map[string]interface{}{"content": content},
    33→		"timestamp": time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
    34→	})
    35→	return b
    36→}
    37→
    38→// AddAssistantMessage adds an assistant message with text content.
    39→func (b *TranscriptBuilder) AddAssistantMessage(content string) *TranscriptBuilder {
    40→	b.messages = append(b.messages, map[string]interface{}{
    41→		"uuid": fmt.Sprintf("asst-%d", len(b.messages)+1),
    42→		"type": "assistant",
    43→		"message": map[string]interface{}{
    44→			"content": []map[string]interface{}{
    45→				{"type": "text", "text": content},
    46→			},
    47→		},
    48→		"timestamp": time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
    49→	})
    50→	return b
    51→}
    52→
    53→// AddToolUse adds a tool use (Write/Edit) to the transcript.
    54→// Returns the tool use ID for use with AddToolResult.
    55→func (b *TranscriptBuilder) AddToolUse(toolName, filePath, content string) string {
    56→	b.toolUseCounter++
    57→	toolUseID := fmt.Sprintf("toolu_%d", b.toolUseCounter)
    58→
    59→	toolUse := map[string]interface{}{
    60→		"type": "tool_use",
    61→		"id":   toolUseID,
    62→		"name": toolName,
    63→		"input": map[string]interface{}{
    64→			"file_path": filePath,
    65→			"content":   content,
    66→		},
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	b.messages = append(b.messages, map[string]interface{}{
    70→		"uuid": fmt.Sprintf("asst-%d", len(b.messages)+1),
    71→		"type": "assistant",
    72→		"message": map[string]interface{}{
    73→			"content": []interface{}{toolUse},
    74→		},
    75→		"timestamp": time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
    76→	})
    77→
    78→	return toolUseID
    79→}
    80→
    81→// AddToolResult adds a tool result for a previous tool use.
    82→func (b *TranscriptBuilder) AddToolResult(toolUseID string) *TranscriptBuilder {

   100→// AddTaskToolUse adds a Task tool invocation (for subagent calls).
   101→// Returns the tool use ID for use with AddTaskToolResult.
   102→func (b *TranscriptBuilder) AddTaskToolUse(toolUseID, prompt string) string {
   103→	if toolUseID == "" {
   104→		b.toolUseCounter++
   105→		toolUseID = fmt.Sprintf("toolu_%d", b.toolUseCounter)
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	toolUse := map[string]interface{}{
   109→		"type": "tool_use",
   110→		"id":   toolUseID,
   111→		"name": "Task",
   112→		"input": map[string]interface{}{
   113→			"prompt":        prompt,
   114→			"subagent_type": "general-purpose",
   115→		},
   116→	}
   117→
   118→	b.messages = append(b.messages, map[string]interface{}{
   119→		"uuid": fmt.Sprintf("asst-%d", len(b.messages)+1),
   120→		"type": "assistant",
   121→		"message": map[string]interface{}{
   122→			"content": []interface{}{toolUse},
   123→		},
   124→		"timestamp": time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
   125→	})
   126→
   127→	return toolUseID
   128→}
   129→
   130→// AddTaskToolResult adds a tool result for a Task tool invocation.
   131→// The UUID of this message is used as the checkpoint UUID for rewind.
   132→func (b *TranscriptBuilder) AddTaskToolResult(toolUseID, agentID string) string {
   133→	uuid := fmt.Sprintf("user-%d", len(b.messages)+1)
   134→
   135→	b.messages = append(b.messages, map[string]interface{}{
   136→		"uuid": uuid,
   137→		"type": "user",
   138→		"message": map[string]interface{}{
   139→			"content": []map[string]interface{}{
   140→				{
   141→					"type":        "tool_result",
   142→					"tool_use_id": toolUseID,
   143→					"content":     "agentId: " + agentID,
   144→				},
   145→			},
   146→		},
   147→		"timestamp": time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
   148→	})
   149→
   150→	return uuid
   151→}
   152→
   153→// WriteToFile writes the transcript to a JSONL file.
   154→func (b *TranscriptBuilder) WriteToFile(path string) error {
   155→	// Ensure directory exists
   156→	dir := filepath.Dir(path)
   157→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o755); err != nil {
   158→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create directory: %w", err)
   159→	}
   160→
   161→	file, err := os.Create(path)
   162→	if err != nil {
   163→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create file: %w", err)
   164→	}
   165→	defer func() { _ = file.Close() }()
   166→
   167→	encoder := json.NewEncoder(file)
   168→	for _, msg := range b.messages {
   169→		if err := encoder.Encode(msg); err != nil {
   170→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to encode message: %w", err)
   171→		}
   172→	}
   173→	return nil
   174→}
   175→
   176→// String returns the transcript as a JSONL string.
   177→func (b *TranscriptBuilder) String() string {
   178→	var result string
   179→	var resultSb176 strings.Builder
   180→	for _, msg := range b.messages {
   181→		data, _ := json.Marshal(msg)
   182→		resultSb176.WriteString(string(data) + "\n")
   183→	}
   184→	result += resultSb176.String()
   185→	return result
   186→}
   187→
   188→// LastUUID returns the UUID of the last message added.
   189→func (b *TranscriptBuilder) LastUUID() string {
   190→	if len(b.messages) == 0 {
   191→		return ""
   192→	}
   193→	return b.messages[len(b.messages)-1]["uuid"].(string)
   194→}
   195→

    22→// HookNames returns the hook verbs Factory AI Droid supports.
    23→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) HookNames() []string {
    24→	return f.GetHookNames()
    25→}
    26→
    27→// ParseHookEvent translates a Factory AI Droid hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
    28→// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance.
    29→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    30→	switch hookName {
    31→	case HookNameSessionStart:
    32→		return f.parseSessionStart(stdin)
    33→	case HookNameUserPromptSubmit:
    34→		return f.parseTurnStart(stdin)
    35→	case HookNameStop:
    36→		return f.parseTurnEnd(stdin)
    37→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
    38→		return f.parseSessionEnd(stdin)
    39→	case HookNamePreToolUse:
    40→		return f.parseSubagentStart(stdin)
    41→	case HookNamePostToolUse:
    42→		return f.parseSubagentEnd(stdin)
    43→	case HookNamePreCompact:
    44→		return f.parseCompaction(stdin)
    45→	case HookNameSubagentStop, HookNameNotification:
    46→		// Acknowledged hooks with no lifecycle action
    47→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action
    48→	default:
    49→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle action
    50→	}
    51→}
    52→
    53→// --- TranscriptAnalyzer ---
    54→
    55→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current line count of the JSONL transcript.
    56→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
    57→	_, pos, err := ParseDroidTranscript(path, 0)
    58→	if err != nil {
    59→		return 0, err
    60→	}
    61→	return pos, nil
    62→}
    63→
    64→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given line offset.
    65→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) ([]string, int, error) {
    66→	lines, currentPos, err := ParseDroidTranscript(path, startOffset)
    67→	if err != nil {
    68→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
    69→	}
    70→	files := ExtractModifiedFiles(lines)
    71→	return files, currentPos, nil
    72→}
    73→
    74→// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given line offset.
    75→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {
    76→	lines, _, err := ParseDroidTranscript(sessionRef, fromOffset)
    77→	if err != nil {
    78→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
    79→	}
    80→
    81→	var prompts []string
    82→	for i := range lines {
    83→		if lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeUser {
    84→			continue
    85→		}
    86→		content := transcript.ExtractUserContent(lines[i].Message)
    87→		if content != "" {
    88→			prompts = append(prompts, textutil.StripIDEContextTags(content))
    89→		}
    90→	}
    91→	return prompts, nil
    92→}
    93→
    94→// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message as a session summary.
    95→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {
    96→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    97→	if err != nil {
    98→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    99→	}
   100→	lines, err := ParseDroidTranscriptFromBytes(data)
   101→	if err != nil {
   102→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   103→	}
   104→
   105→	for i := len(lines) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   106→		if lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeAssistant {
   107→			continue
   108→		}
   109→		var msg transcript.AssistantMessage
   110→		if err := json.Unmarshal(lines[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {
   111→			continue
   112→		}
   113→		for _, block := range msg.Content {
   114→			if block.Type == transcript.ContentTypeText && block.Text != "" {
   115→				return block.Text, nil
   116→			}
   117→		}
   118→	}
   119→	return "", nil
   120→}
   121→
   122→// --- TokenCalculator ---
   123→
   124→// CalculateTokenUsage computes token usage from the transcript starting at the given line offset.
   125→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   126→	return CalculateTotalTokenUsageFromTranscript(sessionRef, fromOffset, "")
   127→}
   128→
   129→// --- SubagentAwareExtractor ---
   130→
   131→// ExtractAllModifiedFiles extracts files modified by both the main agent and any spawned subagents.
   132→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ExtractAllModifiedFiles(sessionRef string, fromOffset int, subagentsDir string) ([]string, error) {
   133→	return ExtractAllModifiedFilesFromTranscript(sessionRef, fromOffset, subagentsDir)
   134→}
   135→
   136→// CalculateTotalTokenUsage computes token usage including all spawned subagents.
   137→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) CalculateTotalTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int, subagentsDir string) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   138→	return CalculateTotalTokenUsageFromTranscript(sessionRef, fromOffset, subagentsDir)
   139→}

   141→// --- Internal hook parsing functions ---
   142→
   143→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseSessionStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   144→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   145→	if err != nil {
   146→		return nil, err
   147→	}
   148→	return &agent.Event{
   149→		Type:       agent.SessionStart,
   150→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   151→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   152→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   153→	}, nil
   154→}
   155→
   156→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseTurnStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   157→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[userPromptSubmitRaw](stdin)
   158→	if err != nil {
   159→		return nil, err
   160→	}
   161→	return &agent.Event{
   162→		Type:       agent.TurnStart,
   163→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   164→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   165→		Prompt:     raw.Prompt,
   166→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   167→	}, nil
   168→}
   169→
   170→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseTurnEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   171→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   172→	if err != nil {
   173→		return nil, err
   174→	}
   175→	return &agent.Event{
   176→		Type:       agent.TurnEnd,
   177→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   178→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   179→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   180→	}, nil
   181→}
   182→
   183→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseSessionEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   184→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   185→	if err != nil {
   186→		return nil, err
   187→	}
   188→	return &agent.Event{
   189→		Type:       agent.SessionEnd,
   190→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   191→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   192→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   193→	}, nil
   194→}
   195→
   196→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseSubagentStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   197→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[taskHookInputRaw](stdin)
   198→	if err != nil {
   199→		return nil, err
   200→	}
   201→	return &agent.Event{
   202→		Type:       agent.SubagentStart,
   203→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   204→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   205→		ToolUseID:  raw.ToolUseID,
   206→		ToolInput:  raw.ToolInput,
   207→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   208→	}, nil
   209→}
   210→
   211→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseSubagentEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   212→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[postToolHookInputRaw](stdin)
   213→	if err != nil {
   214→		return nil, err
   215→	}
   216→	event := &agent.Event{
   217→		Type:       agent.SubagentEnd,
   218→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   219→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   220→		ToolUseID:  raw.ToolUseID,
   221→		ToolInput:  raw.ToolInput,
   222→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   223→	}
   224→	if raw.ToolResponse.AgentID != "" {
   225→		event.SubagentID = raw.ToolResponse.AgentID
   226→	}
   227→	return event, nil
   228→}
   229→
   230→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) parseCompaction(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   231→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   232→	if err != nil {

   234→	}
   235→	return &agent.Event{

   237→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
The integration tests use a **compiled test binary** pattern: `TestMain` builds the CLI once, then all tests invoke it as a subprocess. This means hook runners don't call Go functions directly — they shell out to `getTestBinary() hooks <agent> <hook-name>` with JSON on stdin. This is what makes these "integration" tests rather than unit tests — they exercise the full binary path including command routing and agent dispatch.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

Let me start with Step 1 — adding the FactoryDroidHookRunner and related helpers to `hooks.go`.

{\"type\":\"assistant\",\"uuid\":\"...\",\"message\":{\"content\":[...]}}.\nfunc (s *FactoryDroidSession) CreateDroidTranscript(prompt string, changes []FileChange) string {\n\tvar lines []map[string]interface{}\n\n\t// User message with prompt\n\tlines = append(lines, map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\"type\": \"message\",\n\t\t\"id\":   \"m1\",\n\t\t\"message\": map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\t\"role\": \"user\",\n\t\t\t\"content\": []map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\t\t{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": prompt},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t},\n\t})\n\n\t// Assistant message with tool uses\n\tassistantContent := []interface{}{\n\t\tmap[string]interface{}{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"I'll help you with that.\"},\n\t}\n\tfor i, change := range changes {\n\t\tassistantContent = append(assistantContent, map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\t\"type\":  \"tool_use\",\n\t\t\t\"id\":    fmt.Sprintf(\"toolu_%d\", i+1),\n\t\t\t\"name\":  \"Write\",\n\t\t\t\"input\": map[string]string{\"file_path\": change.Path, \"content\": change.Content},\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\tlines = append(lines, map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\"type\": \"message\",\n\t\t\"id\":   \"m2\",\n\t\t\"message\": map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\t\"role\":    \"assistant\",\n\t\t\t\"content\": assistantContent,\n\t\t},\n\t})\n\n\t// Tool results\n\ttoolResultContent := make([]map[string]interface{}, 0, len(changes))\n\tfor i := range changes {\n\t\ttoolResultContent = append(toolResultContent, map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\t\"type\":        \"tool_result\",\n\t\t\t\"tool_use_id\": fmt.Sprintf(\"toolu_%d\", i+1),\n\t\t\t\"content\":     \"Success\",\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\tlines = append(lines, map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\"type\": \"message\",\n\t\t\"id\":   \"m3\",\n\t\t\"message\": map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\t\"role\":    \"user\",\n\t\t\t\"content\": toolResultContent,\n\t\t},\n\t})\n\n\t// Final assistant message\n\tlines = append(lines, map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\"type\": \"message\",\n\t\t\"id\":   \"m4\",\n\t\t\"message\": map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\t\"role\": \"assistant\",\n\t\t\t\"content\": []map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\t\t{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"Done!\"},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t},\n\t})\n\n\t// Ensure directory exists\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(s.TranscriptPath), 0o755); err != nil {\n\t\ts.env.T.Fatalf(\"failed to create transcript dir: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Write as JSONL\n\tfile, err := os.Create(s.TranscriptPath)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\ts.env.T.Fatalf(\"failed to create transcript file: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer func() { _ = file.Close() }()\n\n\tencoder := Now Step 2 — add Factory AI Droid tests to `agent_test.go`.

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
The detection tests (`TestFactoryAIDroidAgentDetection`) can't use `t.Parallel()` at the top level because `DetectPresence()` internally calls `paths.RepoRoot()` which depends on the working directory. The test needs `os.Chdir()` which is process-global state. However, the "agent is registered" subtest doesn't use `os.Chdir`, so it CAN be parallel. This selective parallelism is a deliberate pattern seen in the existing Claude and Gemini tests.

Now Step 3 — create `setup_factoryai_hooks_test.go`.

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
The `setup_*_hooks_test.go` files are "smoke tests" that exercise the **full CLI binary path**: they run `entire enable --agent factoryai-droid` as a subprocess, then verify the settings file was written correctly. This is different from the `TestFactoryAIDroidHookInstallation` tests above which call `InstallHooks()` directly on the Go struct. The smoke tests catch issues like missing cobra command registration, incorrect agent flag routing, or broken serialization.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

> AGENT

Now Step 4 — add strategy composition tests to `agent_strategy_test.go`.

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
The strategy composition tests can't use `RunForAllStrategies` because that helper hardcodes the Claude Code agent (it uses `NewFeatureBranchEnv` which defaults to claude-code). Instead, we iterate `AllStrategies()` manually and use `InitEntireWithAgent(strat, "factoryai-droid")` to set up the Droid agent. This is important because the lifecycle dispatcher (which reads transcripts and creates checkpoints) uses the agent from `.entire/settings.json` to determine which `TranscriptAnalyzer` to use for parsing.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

  "new_string": "// TestSetupAgentFlag verifies the --agent flag in enable command.\nfunc TestSetupAgentFlag(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\tenv := NewTestEnv(t)\n\tenv.InitRepo()\n\n\t// Run enable with --agent flag\n\toutput := env.RunCLI(\"enable\", \"--agent\", \"claude-code\")\n\tif strings.Contains(output, \"error\") || strings.Contains(output, \"Error\") {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"enable --agent claude-code failed\\nOutput: %s\", output)\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify hooks were installed\n\tsettingsPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, \".claude\", claudecode.ClaudeSettingsFileName)\n\tif _, err := os.Stat(settingsPath); os.IsNotExist(err) {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"enable --agent should create .claude/%s\", claudecode.ClaudeSettingsFileName)\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify .entire/settings has agent set\n\tentireSettingsPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, \".entire\", paths.SettingsFileName)\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(entireSettingsPath)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to read .entire/%s: %v\", paths.SettingsFileName, err)\n\t}\n\n\tif !strings.Contains(string(data), `\"agent\"`) && !strings.Contains(string(data), `\"agent\":`) {\n\t\tt.Logf(\"settings content: %s\", data)\n\t\t// Agent field may be omitted if default\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestFactoryAIDroidAgentStrategyComposition verifies that the Factory AI Droid agent\n// works correctly with each strategy. This tests the full hook-based flow:\n// agent hooks dispatch → lifecycle dispatcher → strategy saves checkpoint.\nfunc TestFactoryAIDroidAgentStrategyComposition(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\t// Iterate strategies manually (can't use RunForAllStrategies — it defaults agent to claude-code)\n\tfor _, strat := range AllStrategies() {\n\t\tstrat := strat // capture for parallel\n\t\tt.Run(strat, func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\t\tt.Parallel()\n\n\t\t\t// Set up repo with the specific strategy and factoryai-droid agent\n\t\t\tenv := NewTestEnv(t)\n\t\t\tenv.InitRepo()\n\t\t\tenv.InitEntireWithAgent(strat, \"factoryai-droid\")\n\n\t\t\t// Create initial commit\n\t\t\tenv.WriteFile(\".gitignore\", \".entire/\\n\")\n\t\t\tenv.WriteFile(\"README.md\", \"# Test Repository\")\n\t\t\tenv.GitAdd(\".gitignore\")\n\t\t\tenv.GitAdd(\"README.md\")\n\t\t\tenv.GitCommit(\"Initial commit\")\n\n\t\t\t// Create feature branch\n\t\t\tenv.GitCheckoutNewBranch(\"feature/droid-test\")\n\n\t\t\t// Create a Droid session with Droid-envelope transcript\n\t\t\tsession := env.NewFactoryDroidSession()\n\t\t\tenv.WriteFile(\"feature.go\", \"package main\\n// new feature\")\n\t\t\tsession.CreateDroidTranscript(\"Add a feature\", []FileChange{\n\t\t\t\t{Path: \"feature.go\", Content: \"package main\\n// new feature\"},\n\t\t\t})\n\n\t\t\t// Simulate session flow: UserPromptSubmit → Stop\n\t\t\tif err := env.SimulateFactoryDroidUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"SimulateFactoryDroidUserPromptSubmit error = %v\", err)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif err := env.SimulateFactoryDroidStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"SimulateFactoryDroidStop error = %v\", err)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Verify checkpoint was created\n\t\t\tpoints := env.GetRewindPoints()\n\t\t\tif len(points) == 0 {\n\t\t\t\tt.Fatal(\"expected at least 1 rewind point after Stop hook\")\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t})\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestFactoryAIDroidSessionIDTransformation verifies session ID transformation and rewind\n// across the agent/strategy boundary for Factory AI Droid.\nfunc TestFactoryAIDroidSessionIDTransformation(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\tfor _, strat := range AllStrategies() {\n\t\tstrat := strat\n\t\tt.Run(strat, func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\t\tt.Parallel()\n\n\t\t\tenv := NewTestEnv(t)\n\t\t\tenv.InitRepo()\n\t\t\tenv.InitEntireWithAgent(strat, \"factoryai-droid\")\n\n\t\t\tenv.WriteFile(\".gitignore\", \".entire/\\n\")\n\t\t\tenv.WriteFile(\"README.md\", \"# Test\")\n\t\t\tenv.GitAdd(\".gitignore\")\n\t\t\tenv.GitAdd(\"README.md\")\n\t\t\tenv.GitCommit(\"Initial commit\")\n\t\t\tenv.GitCheckoutNewBranch(\"feature/droid-rewind\")\n\n\t\t\t// Create session\n\t\t\tsession := env.NewFactoryDroidSession()\n\t\t\tenv.WriteFile(\"test.go\", \"package main\")\n\t\t\tsession.CreateDroidTranscript(\"Test\", []FileChange{\n\t\t\t\t{Path: \"test.go\", Content: \"package main\"},\n\t\t\t})\n\n\t\t\t// Simulate hooks\n\t\t\tif err := env.SimulateFactoryDroidUserPromptSubmit(session.ID); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"UserPromptSubmit error = %v\", err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif err := env.SimulateFactoryDroidStop(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Stop error = %v\", err)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Get rewind points and verify we can rewind\n\t\t\tpoints := env.GetRewindPoints()\n\t\t\tif len(points) == 0 {\n\t\t\t\tt.Skip(\"no rewind points created\")\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Rewind should work\n\t\t\tif err := env.Rewind(points[0].ID); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"Rewind() error = %v\", err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t})\n\t}\n}"
}

The issue is that `InitEntireWithAgent` writes `"agent": "factoryai-droid"` to `.entire/settings.json`, but `EntireSettings` doesn't have that field. The settings loader uses `DisallowUnknownFields()` and rejects it. Let me check how the lifecycle dispatcher determines the agent.

> AGENT

Now I see the issue. `GetStrategy()` (line 70) is called by the `rewind` command. It loads settings, and when settings parsing fails due to the unknown `"agent"` field, it logs a warning and falls back to the default strategy (manual-commit). The log line gets mixed with the JSON output.

The fix: don't write `"agent"` to `.entire/settings.json` in the test helper since the `EntireSettings` struct doesn't recognize it. The agent is determined by the hook command routing (`entire hooks factoryai-droid ...`), not by settings. I should just use `InitEntire(strat)` without agent, since the hooks are called through the `FactoryDroidHookRunner` which already routes to `entire hooks factoryai-droid`.
